# RecruitmentOS requirement register

- **Baseline commit:** `ab00f9dc49fa88156fc38b4c559066d585cbbea9` (branch `release/finish`). The SRS/BRD were written against `d67828e`; this baseline also contains `d5b4c69` (bounded CV reads and extraction cancellation/timeouts).
- **Audit date:** 3 November 2026.
- **Local evidence for this audit:** Node v24.20.0, `npx vitest run` → 34 files, 220 tests passed, 0 skipped. `npm run test:storage` → 52 passed. PGlite, fake storage and fake signers only. No dev server, cloud resource, network provider or real data was used.
- **Status legend:** not started | partial | locally verified | Preview verified | Production verified | blocked | deferred.
  - **locally verified:** a local automated test exercises the acceptance criterion.
  - **partial:** code exists but the criterion is not fully tested, or only part of it is built.
  - **blocked:** progress depends on an external decision or live resource.
  - **Preview verified:** the acceptance criterion is exercised on the Neon Preview database by the owner's run of `scripts/db-role-evidence.ts` and `npm run test:live-roles`; there is still no Production deployment.
  - **Production verified:** none yet, because no Production deployment exists.
- **Reading rule:** a "locally verified" row still needs hosted evidence before any release gate. PGlite cannot prove live Neon privileges, multi-connection races, provider guarantees, delivered email or real browser behaviour (SRS §13.1).

Common path abbreviations: `RS` = `src/components/workspace/records-screen.tsx`, `BCI` = `src/components/workspace/bulk-cv-import.tsx`.

| SRS-TOK-03 | P0       | FR-ADM-02, NFR-10            | DB-backed 60/min counter and per-client invalid-token budget (`api-guess:<client key>`, 10 unknown token hashes per 60 s; revoked, expired, wrong-kind and wrong-audience credentials and non-members charge nothing). Since job 031 (SEC-04) a token whose hash matches a live, active row is admitted even while its client is over budget (tokens carry 256 random bits, so admission is no useful oracle), and every other token from that client gets 429 `token_guess_rate_limited` without growing the count; the in-process pre-check is removed, so each over-budget request costs one indexed identity lookup (`withGuessBudget` in `src/lib/api-tokens.ts`, client IP from `src/lib/client-ip.ts`), `Retry-After: 60` (`errorResponse` in `src/lib/api.ts`), documented on every 429 in `src/app/api/openapi.json/route.ts`; `/api/mcp` uses the same `api-guess:<client key>` budget and 60/min `api-token:<id>` limit for both token kinds; OAuth adds `oauth-register:<client key>` (5/min), a cap of 1,000 clients without a grant, and `oauth-token:<client key>:<client_id>` per registered client or `oauth-token:<client key>` (10 failed/min). Client keys are HMAC-SHA256 of the address under a key derived from `BETTER_AUTH_SECRET` (`rateLimitClientKey` in `src/lib/client-ip.ts`); Better Auth's limiter stores `ba:<hash>` through a `customStorage` (`src/lib/auth.ts`). `purgeExpiredAuthRows` (`src/lib/auth-purge.ts`) deletes spent rate-limit rows after 10 minutes and OAuth codes, tokens and unapproved clients a day after expiry, at most 500 per table and once a minute per instance; the role template grants the identity role `DELETE` on the three OAuth tables | `tests/api-tokens.test.ts` (rejects the 61st request; Retry-After and correlation ID through `GET /api/v1/overview`), `tests/api-token-identity-routing.test.ts`, `tests/openapi-contract.test.ts`, `tests/api-token-gate.test.ts` (job 031: the 11th unknown token gets 429 with `Retry-After: 60`, then a valid token from that client gets 200 and updates `last_used_at`, and the count stays at 10; shared egress over budget admits a live API token and OAuth access token and refuses unknown, revoked, expired, wrong-audience and refresh-row tokens with 429; rotating spoofed headers under `VERCEL=1` keep the budget; each well-formed token is looked up after the readiness and format checks); `tests/api-token-gate.test.ts` also covers hashed keys that change with the secret and stale API and OAuth credentials 20 times each with no charge; `tests/mcp-endpoint.test.ts` (only the unknown token charges; over budget, unknown and revoked tokens get 429 and the count stays at 10; job 031 shared-egress test: a live API token and OAuth access token get 200 on `initialize`; 61st request 429), `tests/mcp-oauth.test.ts` (6th registration and 11th failed token request 429; hashed `oauth-register`/`oauth-token` keys; per-client token budget; the registration cap at 1,000; purge retention, 500-row batches, reuse detection after purge, the 60 s throttle and a missing `DELETE` grant), `tests/auth-lifecycle.test.ts` (`ba:` keys with no address or path), `tests/runtime-role-grants.test.ts` (template matrix) | partial          | There are no additional session, provider, store, download or extraction budgets, and multi-instance behaviour is untested. Live Vercel header behaviour for the invalid-token budget is unverified. Claude.ai and ChatGPT share egress IPs: other clients' unknown guesses can still spend an address's budget, but since job 031 valid tokens behind it keep working. Without the in-process pre-check, an over-budget address costs one identity lookup per request. The OAuth `DELETE` grants need the role template re-applied on Preview and an evidence rerun; until then the OAuth purge logs `auth_purge_failed` with `42501`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Roll-up rule: if every linked SRS ID is locally verified, the row is locally verified. A dominant external decision makes it blocked, and nothing implemented makes it not started. Everything else is partial.

## 3 Contradictions and stale instructions

1. `README.md:3,52` describe "private Amazon S3 CV storage" and point deployment to `docs/s3-setup.md`. The selected direction is private Vercel Blob (SRS §1, AGENTS.md).
2. `docs/environment-isolation.md:5,9-12,22` say "Do not create or connect Blob stores" and plan versioned S3 buckets per environment. The brief (§8.1, §14.1) records separate Blob stores as already created and Blob as the testing direction.
3. `docs/deployment.md` (§3 settings table, §5 "Complete s3-setup.md", launch checks) prescribes S3/OIDC variables for deployment, with no Blob configuration.
4. **Resolved for the code defaults (WP-04, local):** `docs/s3-setup.md` ("Team storage starts at 10 GiB / 10,000 CVs") and the defaults in `src/db/schema.ts` and `drizzle/0001_app.sql` (`max_bytes 10737418240`, `max_files 10000`) conflicted with the 1 GB planning envelope (SRS-CV-07, DEC-05) and the required store-wide allocation. Migration `0007` drops those defaults, clears rows that still held them, and adds the approved store budgets in `storage_ledger.budgets`; team limits now inherit the store budget. `PROJECT_CONTRACT.md` (item 8) is unchanged.
5. `.env.example:54-63` sets `CV_STORAGE_PROVIDER=s3` with AWS variables and no Blob variables, and `src/lib/storage.ts:139-140` defaults to S3 when the variable is unset. The legacy provider is therefore the implicit default rather than an explicitly selected alternative.
6. **Resolved (WP-05 and WP-05b, local):** `docs/security.md` ("Intended connections" and "CV handling") then described the disabled Blob PUT and the disabled server-side Blob API connection (since job 030 both describe the enabled Blob flow over Vercel OIDC), and the 4 MiB chunked-download protocol (SRS-CV-05). The CSP `connect-src` in `next.config.ts` is provider-specific: the S3 host patterns only for `s3`, only the path-scoped `https://vercel.com/api/blob/` for `blob`, and neither for any other value (`tests/csp.test.ts`). It is computed at build time.
7. **Resolved for clients, importer and API docs (WP-05, local):** the reservation tags its upload protocol, and `src/components/cv-upload.tsx`, bulk import, the records-screen download, `scripts/legacy/apply.ts`, `src/app/api/openapi.json/route.ts`, `docs/api-examples.md`, `docs/migration.md:86` and `docs/user-guide.md:17` follow the tag instead of assuming S3. `docs/migration.md:11` and the deployment documents in items 1–3 still describe S3.
8. `PROJECT_CONTRACT.md` (gitignored, local only) still specifies "private AWS S3" and the 10 GiB default as the build contract.
9. `docs/validation.md` reports 196 Vitest / 46 storage tests with one skip. The SRS §13.1 reports 219/52. This audit ran 220/52 with no skip at `ab00f9d`.
10. The SRS/BRD/brief pin baseline `d67828e` and treat the cancellation/timeouts work as "unpushed local". That work is now in history (`d5b4c69`), so the "reported local" caveats are stale for this branch.
11. Owner credentials (local only):
    - `scripts/seed-local.ts:20-63` creates a pre-verified **owner** directly, bypassing the bootstrap/verification flow that SRS-ID-01 requires everywhere else.
    - `:162-163` prints the plaintext password to stdout, which is at odds with SRS-SEC-07.
    - Both are guarded to explicit local PGlite only.
12. Node version:
    - `package.json` `engines >=24 <25`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml:20` and `README.md:18` agree on Node 24.
    - There is no `.nvmrc` or `engine-strict`, and AGENTS.md notes Node 26 is first on PATH. Unprefixed commands silently run outside the supported engine.
    - `@types/node` is the only unpinned devDependency (`^24.0.0`).
13. Viewer personal state: `src/lib/records.ts:1374` requires a write permission for personal follow-up, so viewers cannot keep a shortlist or notes. SRS §2.3 grants viewers personal-interaction exceptions ("Human users may maintain their own shortlist, follow-up…"). Notification read-marking is allowed for viewers.
14. JFIF: BRD FR-CV-02, `src/components/workspace/bulk-cv-queue.ts:10` and `docs/migration.md:110` accept JFIF. The SRS-CV-02 baseline format list omits it.
15. AGENTS.md lists `npm run format:check` as a required check. **Resolved for the format check (job 029, WP-15a, local):** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` now runs `npm run format:check` after `npm run lint`. The git index holds no CRLF files and `npx prettier --check . --end-of-line auto` passes repo-wide, so the check passes on CI's LF checkout. Still open: CI push triggers cover only `main` and `codex/**`, not `release/**` or `factory/**` branches (pull requests trigger CI).
16. **Resolved (WP-02a, local):** the legacy shared-role runtime fixture under `infrastructure/` was removed. `infrastructure/postgres-split-runtime-roles.sql` replaces it. In the owner's run it is applied to the Neon Preview database with zero drift; Production is not verified.
17. **Resolved (WP-01, local):** `docs/security.md` ("Identity and tenancy") now describes the signed per-transaction context and its limits instead of GUC-based isolation.
18. UI controls for disabled features contradict AGENTS.md ("no decorative controls for disabled features") and SRS-ARC-02:
    - **Resolved for the import dialog (WP-09, local):** `bulk-capabilities` now reports `uploadsAvailable: storageConfigured()`, the readiness single CV upload already uses, so Blob and unknown providers stay unavailable. The Import CVs dialog renders its file input, queue, review form and "Import reviewed" button only in the ready state; checking, not set up, unavailable and error show their explanation and Close only (`tests/workspace-render.test.tsx`). The "Import CVs" list button stays as the entry to those explanations.
    - **Resolved for missing storage (WP-05b, local):** `CvUpload` shows a "CV uploads are not set up yet." state with no file input when `bulk-capabilities` reports `storageConfigured:false`.
    - **Resolved (WP-10, job 024, local):** the AI suggestion helpers are wired into the Import CVs dialog, which shows AI controls only when `bulk-capabilities` reports `aiAvailable: true` and the consent endpoint returns a provider disclosure. No provider is approved, so the dialog shows only the "AI extraction is not configured" sentence and no AI control (`tests/workspace-render.test.tsx`).
19. Recovery windows:
    - Idempotency results expire after 7 days. Since WP-06 an expired record-create key reconciles to the existing record (or returns 409 `idempotency_expired`) instead of re-executing the create, so SRS-API-04's no-blind-duplicate rule holds.
    - **Resolved for bulk intake (WP-09, local):** the bulk window is now the server's 24 h recovery metadata (`app.bulk_intake_batches.expires_at`, never extended; an expired batch returns 409 `bulk_batch_expired`), and the UI's 24 h refusal of an uncertain retry matches it. Bulk intake no longer uses `Idempotency-Key`; record-create idempotency stays 7 days.
    - **Updated (job 022, local):** the UI refusal counts from the batch start, taken just before the dialog's first item POST, not from the entry's own start, so a purged batch is never recreated by a late retry. Expired batches and their items are purged, at most 100 per bulk item POST or list GET, across teams.
    - **Updated (job 023, local):** the server now refuses a late retry for any client, browser or API token. The purge deletes a batch's items but keeps its row as a tombstone with `purged_at` (`drizzle/0017_bulk_intake_tombstones.sql`), kept indefinitely like expired idempotency rows, so item and upload POSTs for an expired or purged batch ID return 409 `bulk_batch_expired` and never recreate it, whoever ran the purge and whenever. The client's batch-age refusal stays.

## 4 Proposed work packages

Ordered per SRS §14 sequencing. Each package is independently testable. "Local" means PGlite, fakes and fictional data only. Hosted guards (the hosted sign-in readiness checks in `getAuthOptions()`, the hosted Blob OIDC-only credential checks that refuse a present `BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN`, and the precise hosted database startup refusals) stay in place; a guard is relaxed only when the control it protects is complete and reviewed.

### WP-01 Trusted tenant and identity context

- **Requirements covered:** SRS-SEC-03, SEC-02, SEC-04, DATA-01, ARC-01, CAN-05, TOK-04
- **Files likely touched:**
  - `src/lib/db-team.ts`, `src/db/index.ts`
  - `src/lib/api-tokens.ts`, `src/lib/insights.ts` (activity names), `src/lib/principal.ts`
  - New `drizzle/0006_*.sql` (policies and helpers superseding the GUC-only checks in `0002_rls.sql`)
  - `tests/tenant-context.test.ts`, `tests/token-role-boundary.test.ts`, `tests/activity-role-boundary.test.ts`
- **Local:**
  - Choose and integrate one trusted context mechanism (principal-bound DB identity or signed per-transaction assertion) into every RLS path, including private rows.
  - Close the savepoint-replay gap.
  - Move token create/revoke plus audit into a reviewed identity-side transaction or outbox.
  - Replace the `auth.user` join with a narrow accessor.
  - Prove forged GUCs, replay, expiry and revocation fail under synthetic restricted roles.
- **Done locally:** signed per-transaction assertion in `drizzle/0006_trusted_tenant_context.sql`, `src/lib/tenant-context.ts` and `withTeam`; identity-side token create/revoke with same-transaction audit; activity-name accessor; per-role helper allowlist; adversarial tests in `tests/tenant-context.test.ts` and the rewritten role-boundary tests under synthetic split roles. The research fixtures were removed. The blanket hosted guard stayed closed until WP-02e replaced it with precise startup refusals.
- **BLOCKER:** independent database/security review of the chosen design (D-02, DEC-02, DEC-03).

### WP-02 Restricted-role provisioning and live isolation verification

- **Requirements covered:** SRS-SEC-01, SEC-05, OPS-01, ID-01 (live race), ID-04 (live race), SRS-NFR-09, BRD NFR-01
- **Files likely touched:**
  - New reviewed grant template replacing the legacy shared-role fixture (done: `infrastructure/postgres-split-runtime-roles.sql`)
  - `src/db/connection-boundary.ts`, `src/db/runtime-role.ts`
  - `docs/neon-database-runbook.md`, `docs/preview-database-change-plan.md`
- **Local:** write an object-by-object grant matrix and test it on PGlite with separate identity and data roles. Add role-inspection queries and an evidence script that prints bindings without secrets.
- **Done locally (WP-02a):** `infrastructure/postgres-split-runtime-roles.sql` creates `recruitmentos_data` and `recruitmentos_identity`, grants the exact matrix and closes the database and the `public` schema to PUBLIC in one idempotent transaction; the legacy fixture was removed; `createSplitRoleDb()` provisions its roles with the template; `runtimeRoleSafetyQuery` covers all five protected schemas; `scripts/db-role-evidence.ts` prints read-only role evidence without secrets and exits 1 on drift from the template's matrix, on grant options, parameter and other ACL entries, per-role settings and CONNECT on other databases. Verified on PGlite (`tests/runtime-role-grants.test.ts`) and a throwaway local PostgreSQL 18 cluster. WP-02a applied nothing to a hosted database; the owner has since applied the template to the Neon Preview database (see WP-02e).
- **Done locally (WP-02b):** accepted Neon limitation (owner decision A, 2026-11-04): `scripts/db-role-evidence.ts` accepts another database the runtime roles can connect to, such as Neon's `cloud_admin`-owned `postgres` and `template1`, only when its owner is neither the application database owner nor a runtime role, it is not the application database, the role holds only the PUBLIC CONNECT/TEMPORARY defaults there, and a read-only inspection as the data role finds no protected schema and no `recruitment_migrations` relation; it fails closed and lists accepted databases under `accepted_platform_databases`. Every other database that allows CONNECT still drifts. Verified on PGlite (`tests/runtime-role-grants.test.ts`) and a throwaway local PostgreSQL 18 cluster (defaults accepted with exit 0; exit 1 after `CREATE SCHEMA app` in `postgres`). Nothing was applied to any hosted database.
- **Done locally (WP-02c):** the opt-in harness `scripts/live-role-tests.ts` (`npm run test:live-roles`, excluded from `npm test` and CI) runs against a real PostgreSQL database with the data, identity and owner connections and refuses unless `LIVE_ROLE_TESTS_CONFIRM` equals the database name and the runtime pair, owner and key checks pass. With independent connections and fictional fixtures under a run prefix it covers cross-team reads, writes and guessed identifiers, forged and replayed contexts, savepoint replay, pooled reuse, private rows within a team, data-role identity writes, identity-role candidate reads, revoked and expired tokens, a demoted owner, concurrent last-owner removal, bootstrap claims (open and closed variants), store-budget reservations and read-lease caps, and that denied mutations leave no audit row; it cleans up every fixture and reports counts only. Verified on a throwaway local PostgreSQL 18 cluster; on Neon Preview the owner's run passed 27/27 over direct endpoints and 21/27 with all three connections through the Neon pooler. `tests/live-role-tests.test.ts` covers its refusals.
- **Done locally (WP-02d):** the six checks that failed on the Preview pooler (`bootstrap-claim-race`, `replay-other-connection`, `last-owner-race`, `store-budget-race`, `read-lease-team-cap`, `read-lease-store-cap`) were reproduced exactly with the unmodified harness through PgBouncer 1.26.1 in `pool_mode = transaction` in front of a throwaway PostgreSQL 18 cluster, while the direct run passed 27/27. Root cause: a harness assumption under transaction pooling. The harness captured each client's backend pid once, in an autocommit statement at start-up, and used it for distinctness and `pg_locks` overlap; behind a transaction pooler that pid need not be the backend of a later transaction. Every outcome, positive control and end state of those checks held. No application defect was found: tenant context, the setup lock, ledger and lease locks are all transaction-scoped. The harness now reads every pid it uses inside the transaction concerned (`pooled-connection-reuse` included), and `LIVE_ROLE_TESTS_DIAGNOSE=1` adds a `diagnostics` entry (check, phase, SQLSTATE or fixed category) per failed check. The fixed harness passed 27/27 through the pooler and directly, in the open and closed variants, with every fixture removed. Pooler requirements are in `docs/neon-database-runbook.md` §4.
- **Done locally (WP-02e):** evidence from the owner's run: on the Neon Preview database the role template is applied, `scripts/db-role-evidence.ts` passes with zero drift, and `npm run test:live-roles` passes 27/27 through the transaction-mode pooler and 27/27 direct, with all fixtures removed. `createDbPair()` (`src/db/index.ts`) no longer throws the blanket hosted `tenant_isolation_pending` error. Every existing fail-closed check stays, and hosted startup now also refuses `ALLOW_LOCAL_DB=true` (`local_db_disabled`), `DEV_MAIL_MODE=console` and any `DEV_SEED_PASSWORD` (`development_setting_refused`) and a missing or malformed `TENANT_CONTEXT_SIGNING_KEY` (`tenant_context_unavailable`), before any pool exists. `runtimeRoleSafetyQuery` (`src/db/runtime-role.ts`) also rejects a role that owns, or is a member of the owner of, the current database (NOINHERIT included). No environment variable bypasses these checks. An uninstalled key is still rejected per transaction by `tenant_context.install` (403 `tenant_context_denied`), not at startup. Tests: `tests/connection-boundary-boot.test.ts` (mocked pools: the split-role baseline starts under `NODE_ENV=production` and `VERCEL=1`, one test per refusal, `ALLOW_UNSAFE_TENANT_ISOLATION` inert) and `tests/runtime-role-safety.test.ts` (database owner on PGlite).
- **BLOCKERS:**
  - Production Neon owner connection held by the operator, and human approval to create roles there.
  - Multi-connection race tests (bootstrap, last owner, quota): passed on Preview directly and through the transaction-mode pooler; Production remains. Concurrent token caps are not in the harness.
  - The WP-02e database-owner clause in `runtimeRoleSafetyQuery` has not been run against Preview.
  - Guard removal (WP-02e) awaits the owner's release-gate sign-off; GATE-1 also needs independent review (DEC-02/DEC-03).

### R2a-09 MCP tools and OAuth consent on the engine (job 055)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item R2a-09 (epics R2-06 and R2-09; architecture [§3.2](../release-2/architecture.md#32-one-authorization-function) the MCP row of the call-site table and how rules combine, [§3.4](../release-2/architecture.md#34-field-redaction-in-every-output) the MCP results row and [§11.5](../release-2/architecture.md#115-prompt-injection-through-configurable-labels): tool descriptions stay static and record text reaches MCP only inside the untrusted-data wrapper; owner decisions D4, D13, D14 and D16 of 8 November 2026), toward BRD FR-ACC-03 and SRS-SEC-03, plus the job 052 follow-up: the tool list is built from the stored scopes and the role's actions, so it never offers a tool the call refuses. Nothing changes for the four built-in templates; OAuth consent keeps offering `MCP_SCOPES` the member's team role holds. No migration, SQL, grant, role template, storage schema marker, OAuth, scope-text, tool name, description, input, annotation or `INSTRUCTIONS` change; the `tenant_context.audit_mcp_call` allowlist stays the 59 tool names. Directory and plan data stay off MCP (D13); `SENSITIVE_CANDIDATE_FIELDS` stay excluded for every role (D14). No row status changes.
- **Files touched:** `src/lib/mcp/tools.ts` (each of the 59 tools declares `access`, the engine requests it needs beside its unchanged `scopes`; new exported `toolAllowed(ctx, definition)`, `INPUT_CHECKS` and `inputAllowed`; `allowed()`, the `requireScope` loop and the five inline `requireScope` calls removed, the per-input checks decided on the call's context with today's `Permission <scope> is required.`; `callTool` builds `accessContext(p, access, { channel: "mcp" })` inside its audit transaction, decides, then writes `tenant_context.audit_mcp_call`, and passes the context to `run`; `get_candidate` reads `cvs:read` from the context; `listTools(p, access?)` decides on the engine, and the new `listStoredTools(p)` reads the access state in one `withTeam` with no statement beyond the install; the projections leave out every key `hiddenKeys` hides, with no interview time or meeting-link flag derived from a hidden field, no evidence count from a hidden source field and no hidden contact field in a duplicate match), `src/lib/mcp/server.ts` (`tools/list` answers with `listStoredTools`; an error from its transaction goes through `errorResponse`, so it is logged as a REST error is and the JSON-RPC error carries only the envelope's message, with its code as `data.code`), `src/lib/authz.ts` (comment only), `docs/mcp.md` ("Tools and the scope each needs"), `docs/security.md` ("Identity and tenancy" and "Scope narrowing on every surface"), `docs/release-2/architecture.md` (§3.1 MCP sentence in the past tense) and this entry.
- **Tests:** `tests/authz.test.ts` (new "MCP tools on the engine (R2a-09)" block: 59 tools, each with scopes and an access declaration; for owner, admin, recruiter and viewer with each of the 64 `MCP_SCOPES` subsets as an API token, every tool's decision equals today's scope check; the five per-input checks equal today's `requireScope` wherever their tool runs; no request names a resource outside `resourceToKind`, a company area or the `directory`, `plan` or `admin` page; a role with vacancies `view` and `export` only refuses `create_vacancy`, `update_vacancy` and `archive_record` and allows `list_vacancies`, and a role without the `activity` page refuses `list_activity`), `tests/member-roles.test.ts` (additions only: the narrowed recruiter's tool list has no CV upload or bulk import tool although its token asserts `cvs:write`, and `upload_cv` is refused `scope_required` with one `mcp.tool_denied` row and no upload; a published vacancy-view-only role lists `list_vacancies` but not `create_vacancy` or `update_vacancy`, and `create_vacancy` is refused, audited `mcp.tool_denied` and creates nothing; the pageless role has no `get_overview`, `get_analytics` or `list_activity`; the redacted role gets no `residenceEmirate` in `search_candidates`, no `email` in `get_candidate` facts, no `department` in `list_vacancies`, no `stage` in `list_applications` and no `title` in `list_tasks`, absent rather than `null`, while the owner token gets them; a role hiding interview `startAt` and `meetingUrl` gets `list_interviews` items without `startAt`, `startAtDubai`, `timezone` or `hasMeetingUrl`), `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` (one new case: `listStoredTools` for an owner API token lists 59 tools in at most 6 round trips), `tests/mcp-list-errors.test.ts` (new, no database: a failed install in `tools/list` answers JSON-RPC `-32603` with the generic `internal_error` message and no text of the database error, logging only the correlation id, category and SQLSTATE; a refusal keeps its own code and message as `-32600`). `tests/mcp-endpoint.test.ts`, `tests/mcp-pipeline-tools.test.ts`, `tests/mcp-candidate-task-tools.test.ts`, `tests/mcp-cv-tools.test.ts`, `tests/mcp-oauth.test.ts` and `tests/candidate-erasure.test.ts` are unchanged and pass; the 6, 11 and 15 budgets hold (cold, warm and assigned role).
- **Local:** done with Node 24 on PGlite with fictional data; the full validation suite passes in the worktree. `tools/list` for an owner API token measures 4 round trips after token authentication (`BEGIN`, deadlines and snapshot, install, `COMMIT`); `tools/call` adds none. A throwaway comparison (not committed) on one fictional team ran `tools/list` and all 59 tools, in order and with the same arguments, for owner, admin, recruiter and viewer API tokens holding their roles' MCP scopes, on `main` and on this branch: apart from generated ids, timestamps and the wall-clock stage-duration fractions of `get_analytics`, the 240 results (including 35 `scope_required` refusals for the viewer) were identical.
- **Owner steps:** none to deploy. Open for review: tools keep `scopes` beside `access` and need both, CV scopes checked against the engine's effective scopes (spec assumption 1); every MCP refusal, whether a scope, an action or a page is missing, keeps `scope_required` and today's message (assumption 2); `move_application_stage`, archive and restore are decided as `edit`, as the services decide them (assumption 3); only `get_overview`, `get_analytics` and `list_activity` name pages, so `open_bulk_import_page` does not need the `candidates` page (assumption 4); task workflow tools need buckets and task fields both, and archive and restore need edit on all eight pipeline resources, so a role lacking one loses the tool (assumption 5); `upload_cv`, `create_cv_upload_link` and `complete_cv_upload` are decided by `cvs:write` alone (assumption 6); a hidden field is left out, not `null` (assumption 7); the `tools/list` budget is counted after token authentication (assumption 8); `listTools(p)` without an access state decides under the principal's built-in template, for in-process callers only (assumption 9); the per-input checks reuse the audit transaction's context (assumption 10). Because the dispatcher no longer calls `requireScope`, a CV scope (`cvs:read`, `cvs:write`) is decided once in the audit transaction and is not bound again in the CV service's own transaction, which does not decide it itself, so a role narrowed between the two transactions of one call is not refused there. `find_duplicate_candidates` also leaves out a hidden `email`, `phone` or `name` of a match. `update_task` with `customValues` merges into the redacted stored values, so under a role hiding a custom field the merge cannot keep that hidden value; field rules stay service-layer (D4, D16). Open items: refusing filters and sorts on hidden fields, per-connection limits (architecture §8), record scope (R2a-18).

Open for review: page checkboxes cover every screen that exists, not `approvals`, `directory` or `plan` (spec assumption 7); the editor offers every action of `ACTIONS` per resource except `plans`, `erase` and `admin` on `two-step-reset` and `teams`, as the spec lists, although `authorize` acts only on `admin` for the three team-administration resources, so the other actions there are stored but change nothing (assumption 6); all five grantable areas are offered although only `organisation` has a screen (assumption 9); plan access can only be given with a company level, because the helper requires one (assumption 9); the person select lists only the current team's members and existing holders (assumption 10); the member's plain state is the refusal message, not a new screen (assumption 11); Team settings reads `GET /api/v1/me/access` once more per load (assumption 14).
- **Open escalation (spec assumption 1):** company owners cannot list authority changes workspace-wide, because the runtime role reads only its own team's activity; each change is audited in the acting owner's team. The owner decides whether to queue this migration (next number `0036`); it was not built:

  ```sql
  -- Company owner sessions only (42501 otherwise, 22023 for a page size outside 1-100): the
  -- workspace-authority rows of every team mapped to the context's workspace, newest first. Time,
  -- affected user id, before and after grants, and the actor's name only.
  CREATE FUNCTION tenant_context.workspace_authority_changes(before timestamptz, page_size integer)
  RETURNS TABLE (occurred_at timestamptz, affected_user_id text, before_grant jsonb, after_grant jsonb, actor_name text)
  LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = pg_catalog, pg_temp AS $$
  DECLARE
    ctx tenant_context.contexts%ROWTYPE;
  BEGIN
    SELECT * INTO ctx FROM tenant_context.contexts c
      WHERE c.pid = pg_backend_pid() AND c.xid = pg_current_xact_id_if_assigned()
        AND c.expires_at > clock_timestamp() AND c.auth_type = 'session';
    IF NOT FOUND OR ctx.company_level IS DISTINCT FROM 'owner' OR ctx.workspace_id IS NULL THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Workspace authority changes rejected' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
    END IF;
    IF workspace_authority_changes.page_size IS NULL OR workspace_authority_changes.page_size NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 100 THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Workspace authority changes rejected' USING ERRCODE = '22023';
    END IF;
    RETURN QUERY SELECT a.created_at, a.metadata->>'targetUserId', a.metadata->'before', a.metadata->'after', u.name
      FROM app.activity a
      JOIN app.workspace_teams t ON t.team_id = a.team_id
      LEFT JOIN auth."user" u ON u.id = a.actor_id
      WHERE a.kind = 'workspace-authority' AND a.action = 'workspace_authority.changed'
        AND t.workspace_id = ctx.workspace_id
        AND (workspace_authority_changes.before IS NULL OR a.created_at < workspace_authority_changes.before)
      ORDER BY a.created_at DESC, a.id DESC
      LIMIT workspace_authority_changes.page_size;
  END $$;
  REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION tenant_context.workspace_authority_changes(timestamptz, integer) FROM PUBLIC;
  UPDATE db_boundary.storage_schema SET version = 15;
  ```

  It would also need `GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION tenant_context.workspace_authority_changes(timestamp with time zone, integer) TO recruitmentos_data;` in `infrastructure/postgres-split-runtime-roles.sql`, the matching entries in `scripts/runtime-role-template.ts` and `dataRoleHelpers` in `src/db/connection-boundary.ts`, `STORAGE_SCHEMA_VERSION = 15`, and a follow-up adding the rows to `GET /api/v1/access-changes`. Rows written before `0029` carry no `workspaceId`, so the helper scopes by team mapping, which is enough under D15. Other open items: record-scope roles (R2a-18), the other configuration tabs.

- **Follow-up:** the "Takes effect later" labels in the role editor were added in job 058 (R2a-16).

### R2a-16 Organisation tab: org chart and directory (job 058)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item R2a-16 (epics R2-07 and R2-01; architecture [§6.2](../release-2/architecture.md#62-tables-all-proposed-company-level) tables, [§6.3](../release-2/architecture.md#63-org-chart-directory-and-vacancies) org chart, directory and linking to users, [§6.5](../release-2/architecture.md#65-how-record-scope-follows-the-hierarchy) owner-only home-unit changes, [§12](../release-2/architecture.md#12-performance-and-scale-targets) 6 round trips per directory page and org chart level and [§11.6](../release-2/architecture.md#116-configuration-as-a-privilege-escalation-path), with the tenancy decision; owner decisions D1, D13, D15 and D16 of 8 November 2026), toward BRD FR-ADM-01 and SRS-SEC-03, plus the job 056 follow-up ("Takes effect later" in the role editor). ### R2a-17 Excel import for the organisation (job 059)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item R2a-17 (epic R2-07; architecture [§6.4](../release-2/architecture.md#64-excel-import) Excel import, [§6.5](../release-2/architecture.md#65-how-record-scope-follows-the-hierarchy) scope-moving rows need a company owner, [§11.4](../release-2/architecture.md#114-formula-injection-in-exports-and-excel-import) formula injection and [§12](../release-2/architecture.md#12-performance-and-scale-targets) largest configuration, with the tenancy decision; owner decisions D1, D13, D15 and D16 of 8 November 2026), toward BRD FR-ADM-01 and SRS-OPS-03 (dry run before an import). Session-only `POST /api/v1/org-import/dry-run` and `POST /api/v1/org-import/apply?sha256=<hex>[&group=owner]` for company owners and company admins with the organisation area, and an **Import from Excel** panel on the Organisation tab. Built on the existing org and directory checks, columns and refusal mapping, so `app.guard_scope_sources()`, the row guards, `app.maintain_org_closure()`, the audit triggers and RLS guard every imported row. No migration, SQL, policy, grant, role template, storage schema marker, dependency, MCP tool, scope or allowlist change (D13). No row status changes.
- **Files touched:** `src/lib/org-import.ts` (new: body, zip central-directory and row limits, parsing with `read-excel-file`, planning, the read-only dry run, the per-sheet apply and its counts-only audit row), `src/app/api/v1/org-import/dry-run/route.ts` and `src/app/api/v1/org-import/apply/route.ts` (new), `src/lib/org.ts` and `src/lib/directory.ts` (only `export` added to `requireWrite`, `inOrg`, `ownerRequired`, `inDirectory`, `requireDirectory`, `stateConflict` and `leftAt`), `src/domain/org.ts` (`XLSX_TYPE`), `src/lib/insights.ts` (`org-import` in `COMPANY_ACTIVITY_KINDS`), `src/app/api/openapi.json/route.ts` (both paths, `x-session-only`, the raw body as `x-request-body`), `src/components/settings/org-import-panel.tsx` (new), `src/components/settings/organisation-tab.tsx` (renders the panel and reloads the chart after an apply), `src/components/workspace/client.tsx` (`requestJson` sets `Content-Type: application/json` only when the caller set none), `tests/org-import.test.ts` and `tests/org-import-ui.test.tsx` (new), `tests/openapi-contract.test.ts` (two session-only list entries only), `docs/user-guide.md`, `docs/api-examples.md`, this entry.
- **Tests:** `tests/org-import.test.ts` (new, PGlite with the split runtime roles, fictional workbooks built with `write-excel-file` and raw central directories): a dry run with create, update, unchanged and reject counts and a reason for an unknown level, a missing parent, a cyclic parent, a duplicate code, an invalid work email and phone, an unknown position code and more than one primary position, with `app.org_units`, `app.org_unit_closure`, `app.positions`, `app.employees`, `app.employee_positions` and `app.activity` identical before and after; apply writing exactly the counted rows (new employees `source = 'excel'`) and a second apply writing nothing; one `org-import.applied` row per sheet with exactly the seven count keys and no workbook value, seen in the feed by a company owner and not by a team admin without company authority; formula-like cells stored as text and flagged; the owner group listed and never written by a normal apply (owner or company admin), 403 `org_owner_required` for a company admin's owner step, 42501 from `app.guard_scope_sources()` for each sheet when the owner check is bypassed, and the owner step writing the moves with `scope.home_units_changed` rows naming the linked user; 403 `org_forbidden`, `directory_forbidden`, `session_required` and `org_import_forbidden`; 409 `import_file_changed`, 400 `invalid_input` and 415 `xlsx_required`; 413 `import_too_large`, 422 `import_archive_too_large` (2,001 entries, 50 MiB + 1 declared), 422 `import_unreadable` (`../x.xml`, not a zip, a local header declaring 300 MiB, deflated and bit 3 entries inflating past their declared size, a data descriptor signature inside bit 3 data) with `read-excel-file` never called and only the authority-check transaction opened, and a caller without authority refused with 403 before the body is read, and 422 `import_too_many_rows` for 20,001 rows; 2,000 units in 6 levels, 5,000 positions and 5,000 employees dry-run and applied (measured on PGlite: units 2.7 s, positions 7.0 s, employees 4.5 s per transaction as a company owner; 3.6 s, 9.4 s and 5.1 s as a company admin, against the unchanged 20 s deadline). `tests/org-import-ui.test.tsx` (new, no database): the panel for a company owner and a company admin with the area, Check file and Apply sending the raw file with the xlsx content type and the dry run's `sha256`, Apply disabled before a dry run with a change, the lists, and the owner button for company owners only with the admin text. `tests/org-units.test.ts`, `tests/directory.test.ts`, `tests/organisation-ui.test.tsx`, `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` and the MCP suites are unchanged and pass.

In the browser, as the seed company owner: three levels added, a setup workbook checked and applied, then a workbook with creates, an unknown level, an unknown unit code, an invalid email, three formula-like cells and a unit move checked (counts, Rejected rows, Flagged cells and Needs a company owner shown), applied after confirming (new units in the chart, `=Fictional Ops` stored as text), and the owner changes applied after a separate confirmation (the unit moved).
- **Owner steps:** none to deploy beyond the release. Open for review (spec assumptions): the owner step is `group=owner` on the apply route (1); the client sends the file again and apply checks its SHA-256 (2); the owner step applies only owner-group rows, so it follows the normal apply (3); a row with any owner-only change waits wholly for the owner step (4); the 20,000-row cap is checked just after parsing (5); declared zip sizes are no longer only trusted: after the central-directory caps, each local entry must match its directory entry as the parser's streaming reader (unzipper) reads it, and inflate to exactly its declared size, and the directory must end exactly at the end record, which ends the file (rounds 2 and 3, security review) (6); blank cells never clear a value (7); the import never changes or infers a user link (8); apply needs one of `candidates:write`, `recruitment:write`, `cvs:write` or `imports:write` because of the activity insert policy (9, product decision); the audit row is a new `org-import` kind shown only to company authority (10); levels by name, comma or semicolon separators, yes/no/true/false for Manager (11); owner-group membership mirrors the guard's latest-row rule (12); lists capped at 500 (14). Also: a position or unit row that depends on a new unit or position in the owner group is rejected until the owner step has run; whether a position is held by a linked employee is read under the caller's directory RLS, so for a caller without `directory:read` a move of such a position is refused by the database on apply (403 `directory_owner_required`) rather than listed in the owner group; the request body is documented as `x-request-body` because the contract test requires a JSON example for every `requestBody`.

Until `0037` is applied, hosted startup refuses with 503 `storage_schema_mismatch`; until the template is rerun nothing writes assignments, which affects only the assignments service. R2a-19 (job 061, below) adds the database enforcement of record scope and must merge before the R2a Preview checkpoint. Open for review (spec assumptions and build choices): no backfill, so rows written before `0037` reach an assignment on their vacancy only after their next write or a unit change (1); assigned-scope roles see only records assigned through the service until R2a-20 (2); an interviewer assigned only to an interview does not see its candidate (3); assignment kinds and relations are fixed and any relation puts a record in scope (4, 5); home units ignore employee status (6); any member with vacancy create or edit may set any unit of the company (8); `scopedRolesHidden` is removed rather than kept false (10); CV paths are scoped (11); erasure-time removal writes no `assignment.removed` row (12). Build choices: an access state without `recordScope` (tests only; `install` always returns it) takes the record scope of the role the access state names, not an override, so `tests/authz.test.ts` stays unchanged, and a null record scope sees nothing; the helpers share an owner-only worker function no role may execute; uniqueness and task-field compatibility checks stay team-wide so a conflict outside scope is still a 409; a comment on a comment follows its nearest non-comment ancestor; the hiring manager round-trip case adds task and onboarding view to the template, which the overview needs; `RQ012`-`RQ016` are new SQLSTATEs.

- **Local:** done with Node 24 on PGlite with fictional data; the validation suite results are in the job's build record. `npm run test:live-roles` passed 65/65 in both bootstrap variants with no fixture left, directly on a throwaway local PostgreSQL 18 cluster in the worktree `.data` folder on a loopback port, with `scripts/db-role-evidence.ts` at zero drift before and after. With the `app.records` and `app.idempotency` restrictive policies dropped on that cluster, both new checks failed at `outcome`. The cluster was then stopped and deleted. The pooled run was not done: no PgBouncer binary was available and none may be downloaded. Fixture timings are in [validation.md](../validation.md): the overview and the three list screens of a scope-all member are within 10% of `main`, the vacancy board is 11.7% slower (14.5 to 16.2 ms), and a scoped hiring manager's overview and candidate lists take seconds locally, as they already did without the policies.
- **Owner steps:** apply `0038` after `0037` on Preview, rerun the role template, then `scripts/db-role-evidence.ts` (zero drift) and `npm run test:live-roles`, direct and through the pooler. Until `0038` is applied, hosted startup refuses with 503 `storage_schema_mismatch`; until the template is rerun, every request touching the five tables fails. Open for decision: the vacancy board's scope-all db time is 11.7% above `main`, over the 10% bound; the scoped overview and candidate lists were slow on the local fixture, which R2-PERF-01 (job 063, below) addresses. Open for review (spec assumptions): `in_scope` takes two trailing arguments (1); CV uploads use the shared actor key and rows written before `0038` are not rewritten (2); a null stored role version counts as changed (3); replay protection is keyed on the role version only (4); the CV upload rate counts only uploads a scoped member can see (5); team-wide integrity checks narrow under RLS for members whose task scope is not all (6); a scope refusal answers 403 `record_out_of_scope` for callers whose scope is not all, so a unit-scoped member can no longer create a candidate or a vacancy outside their units (7); the `CYCLE` guard rather than a depth bound (8); the benchmark's `--scoped` member (9); no pooled run without a PgBouncer binary (10).

No migration, SQL, role-template, grant, `connection-boundary` or storage schema change; no MCP tool added, renamed or removed; no row status changes.

### R2a-21 Task boards per team and project (job 064)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item R2a-21 (epic R2-12; architecture [§10](../release-2/architecture.md#10-general-team-tasks-migration) task boards per team or project, §1.2 and §1.4 `config_objects.team_id` for `task-board`, and the tenancy decision that team roles stay team roles; owner decision D1 of 8 November 2026). Team owners and admins define their own team's task boards as team-scoped `task-board` configuration objects in the R2a-04 store: a name, an ordered subset of the team's buckets as columns and at most one filter (one vacancy, one linked record, or one value of a dropdown task field). Every team keeps a built-in **All buckets** board with no stored object. The Tasks screen switches boards, and record scope and "Counts as completed" are unchanged. Team task-board bodies hold ids of the team's own records (buckets, a vacancy, a `TASK_LINK_KINDS` record, a task field): a narrow exception to architecture §1.2's "no record ids in configuration" that this item allows, because task-board rows are readable by their own team only and candidate erasure never deletes those kinds. No migration, SQL, policy, grant, role-template, `connection-boundary` or storage schema marker change; no MCP tool added, renamed or removed; no row status changes.

overdue tasks, the open-task count and reminders identical after a board that leaves out the completed bucket, and no task, bucket or task-field row changed), `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` (new case: a Tasks list with `board` is 6 on a cold and a warm cache), `tests/admin-portal-ui.test.tsx` (new cases: the tab only for team owners and admins, All buckets as text, the three state labels, the controls, the refusal texts beside their row and form, the form's columns, move buttons and filter controls), `tests/workspace-render.test.tsx` (new cases: the switcher's label and options and its place in the toolbar, `board` in the list request, a board's columns in board order with an archived bucket left out and its move options, the retired-board notice, the disabled export note). ### R2-PERF-01 Scoped-member speed (job 063)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 backlog item R2-PERF-01 (owner decision of 10 November 2026, "add a separate performance backlog item"), before the R2a Preview checkpoint and after R2a-19. A scoped hiring manager's overview and first-load lists on the local 2,000-candidate fixture go from 14 to 38 s to under 1 s, while every principal sees exactly what `main` shows. The record-scope rule of architecture §3.5 and its row-level security (R2a-19) are unchanged: no policy, helper, grant, role template or `dataRoleHelpers` change. Toward SRS-NFR-01 and the R2a-19 criterion 6 cost bound. No row status changes.
- **Files touched:** `drizzle/0039_candidate_scope_index.sql` (new: only `CREATE INDEX records_candidate_id_idx ON app.records(team_id, id) WHERE kind = 'candidate';`, which `tenant_context.candidate_in_scope` uses to find its candidate; storage schema stays 17), `src/db/schema.ts` (the index), `src/lib/authz.ts` (`scopedIds`: the team's records of a kind in scope as a union of single-set branches; `scopeRule`, `recordScopeRule` and `scopeFilter` take `{ once: true }` and then test the row's id against those ids, computed once per statement and applied before the policy; the per-row form is unchanged and stays the default), `src/lib/insights.ts` (the overview's counts, coverage, stages, groups, upcoming interviews and overdue tasks take the once form; `inventory` is one subquery per kind; the recent activity keeps the per-row rule), `src/lib/records.ts` (list and export queries and candidate suggestions take the once form), `docs/validation.md` (cause, before/after tables, method), `docs/deployment.md` (the `0039` paragraph), `docs/identity-boundary-plan.md` and `docs/security.md` (the index and the service rule's set form), this entry.
- **Tests:** `tests/record-scope-speed.test.ts` (new: the benchmark fixture built set-based on `createSplitRoleDb()`, and a hiring manager set up as `ensureScoped` sets one up. As the data role, `overview` and the ten first-load lists of `tabRequests()` plus the vacancy board each take under 5,000 ms. The member sees a non-empty strict subset of the fixture's candidates and applications, and the overview counts match the lists. 14/14 pass in about 8 s; against `main`'s code 5 of 14 fail, at 5.9 to 42 s). `tests/record-scope-rls.test.ts` (one new case: as the superuser, the once form selects exactly the per-row form's records of every scoped kind for every scoped principal; every earlier case and assertion is unchanged). `tests/migration-upgrade.test.ts` ("upgrade from 0038 to the candidate scope index": the index definition, rows, marker 17, the five restrictive policies and the five helpers unchanged, and a second run with no change). `tests/org-scope.test.ts`, `tests/authz.test.ts` and `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` are unchanged and pass.
- **Local:** done with Node 24 on PGlite with fictional data. `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm test` (105 files, 1,592 tests), `npm run test:storage`, `npm run build` and Prettier on every changed file pass in the worktree. `npm run bench:round-trips -- --fixture --scoped`: the overview takes 527 to 713 ms total (it took 37,498 and 38,436), the candidates list 131 to 139 ms (18,351), the source-review list 135 to 147 ms (5,529) and every list at most 155.2 ms. Every round-trip count equals `main`'s, and the other scoped rows stay within the run-to-run noise. As the scope-all owner, the overview and every list are within 10% of `main` over three alternating pairs. An earlier nine-pair series had five 6 to 10 ms lists above 10% (by 0.7 to 2.0 ms) with statements identical to `main`'s; a swap control showed them as noise ([validation.md](../validation.md)). `npm run test:live-roles` passed 65/65 in both bootstrap variants on a throwaway local PostgreSQL 18 cluster in the worktree `.data` folder. `scripts/db-role-evidence.ts` showed zero drift before and after, and the cluster was then stopped and deleted. No pooled run was done (no PgBouncer binary).
- **Owner steps:** apply `0039` after `0038` on Preview; the role template is unchanged and needs no rerun. The approver escalates the migration to the owner with its exact SQL: `CREATE INDEX records_candidate_id_idx ON app.records(team_id, id) WHERE kind = 'candidate';`. Without it the code is correct, only slower for scoped members; startup does not refuse a database without it. Open for review: the once form costs a list filtered to a few rows (one vacancy's board) one set of the whole scope's applications, so the board measured 108.7 and 149.0 ms against 112.9 on `main`. Hosted timings are not measured, because no remote database may be used.

### ADM-01 Admin sidebar shell and Admin home (job 066)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item ADM-01 (backlog `R2-ADM-01`, milestone M1, epic R2-01; spec IDs UI-001, UI-003, UI-010, UI-011, PRM-011, PRM-012, IMP-004 and TST-002; architecture [§14.1](../release-2/architecture.md#141-admin-shell-and-sitemap-under-appadmin); owner decisions D1, D20 and D29, option A of 10 November 2026). `/app/admin` shows the Admin home, one card per page the caller can open, instead of jumping to the first tab, and a grouped sidebar replaces the tab row: Overview (Admin home), People (Members, CV storage, Speed, Task boards, Organisation, Directory), Access (Roles & permissions), Integrations (API tokens) and Monitoring (History), with any group the caller has no page in hidden and no entry for a page not built. `ADMIN_TABS` stays the one page table, one level per page, now with a group and a description, and `adminTabs()` keeps its rule. Both admin page files now check access on the server before rendering, through the same `authorize()` answers as `GET /api/v1/me/access`, whose response is unchanged. No area, migration, route, redirect or Company authority change; no row status changes.
- **Files touched:** `src/components/settings/admin-tabs.ts` (`NO_ACCESS` moved here from `admin-portal.tsx` so server pages can import it; `ADMIN_GROUPS`; each row's `group` and `description`, rows ordered by group), `src/components/settings/admin-portal.tsx` (re-exports `NO_ACCESS` and `MeAccess`; no redirect; the `admin-shell` with the sidebar `nav` and the Admin home; the team panels read their text from the table's descriptions), `src/lib/authz.ts` (`MeAccess` and `meAccess()`: the team role, company level and `accessMap()`), `src/app/api/v1/me/access/route.ts` (builds its body with `meAccess()`), `src/lib/admin-access.ts` (new: `adminPageAccess()`, the session from cookies through `getWorkspaceContext()`, one `withTeam` and `meAccess()`), `src/app/app/admin/page.tsx` and `src/app/app/admin/[tab]/page.tsx` (await it; the no-access state when the caller has no team or the page is not theirs), `src/styles/settings.css` (sidebar, cards and the 1100 px stacking rule, theme variables only), `docs/user-guide.md`, `docs/accessibility-checklist.md`, this entry.
- **Tests:** `tests/admin-portal-ui.test.tsx` (extended: the table's keys, labels, groups and order and `ADMIN_GROUPS`; a pure matrix over six team roles, three company levels, all 512 subsets of `ADMIN_AREAS` and four directory action sets; the home's cards, links and group headings for six callers and the no-access home; the sidebar's groups, links, one `aria-current="page"` and no link to any unbuilt route for six callers on every page they hold; existing expectations follow the new order and selectors). `tests/admin-routes.test.ts` (new, PGlite split roles, fictional ids: walks `src/app/app/admin` and fails naming any page file outside the table; calls each page file's default export for every key as anonymous, a Bearer token alone (both redirect to `/sign-in`, with no `withTeam` and no token authenticated), a team owner and a team admin without company authority, a recruiter, a company admin with and without the Organisation area, a company owner who is team owner and one who is recruiter, and a member of no team; an allowed page renders `AdminPortal` with no `NO_ACCESS`, a refused one `AdminPortalView` with only the heading and `NO_ACCESS`, no sidebar; an unknown key is not found). `tests/organisation-ui.test.tsx` (its no-sidebar check looks for the nav's label instead of the old `settings-nav` class). `tests/admin-portal-routes.test.ts` and `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` pass unchanged.
- **Local:** done with Node 24 on PGlite with fictional data; see the job's `build.md` for the validation commands. In the browser as the fictional seed owner (team owner and company owner) at 1280×800, 768×1024 and 360×740 in both themes: `/app/admin` stays at `/app/admin` with nine cards; the sidebar shows the five groups in order; Members, History, Roles & permissions, Organisation, Directory and Task boards open with their content and their own link current; `/app/admin/fictional` shows the app's not-found page; a request with only a Bearer token is redirected to `/sign-in`. See the accessibility checklist.
- **Owner steps:** none to deploy: no migration. Open for review (the spec's assumptions): a refused page renders the plain no-access state, not an HTTP 403 document, as R2a-11 decided, since the workspace `loading.tsx` streams first and `forbidden()` is experimental; the 403 stays on the data routes (1). Members, CV storage, Speed and Task boards sit in People, API tokens in Integrations, History in Monitoring, following the §14.1 page each becomes (2). Labels stay as today, with no team name in the sidebar (3). The home is the one page outside the table, open to anyone with at least one page (4). A Bearer token is anonymous to admin pages, so it is sent to `/sign-in` rather than refused (5). Each admin page load costs one more `withTeam` for the server check, and the client still loads `GET /api/v1/me/access` for the sidebar and the Roles refresh; the measured API requests (6, 11 and 15) are unchanged (6). 768 px is recorded as 768×1024 in the checklist (7).

### R2b-02 Record pins and custom values on every configurable kind (job 067)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item R2b-02 (milestone M5 "Fields and screens", epic R2-02, spec ID FLD-014; architecture [§1.3](../release-2/architecture.md#13-versioning-drafts-and-pinning) pinning, [§1.5](../release-2/architecture.md#15-dynamic-validation-with-the-strict-schemas-authoritative) items 1 and 2 and [§2.3](../release-2/architecture.md#23-the-in-place-migration); owner decision D1). Records gain nullable `schema_version_id` and `type_id`; every create and data update stores the kind schema version it was validated against (the workspace's current published kind schema, or the fixed default id `…201` to `…209`), and buckets, task fields, saved views and comments stay null. Candidates, job roles, vacancies, applications, interviews, offers, onboarding records and checklist items take an optional `customValues` of the task shape over REST, not checked against any field until R2b-04. Existing rows read and write unchanged, never gaining `customValues`; REST bodies of existing records, OpenAPI, MCP tool contracts, forms, record views, exports and the roles tab rows are as on `main`. No row status changes.
- **Files touched:** `drizzle/0040_record_pins.sql` (new: the two columns with `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`, no row rewritten, storage schema 18; role template unchanged), `src/db/schema.ts` (the two columns), `src/db/storage-schema.ts` (`STORAGE_SCHEMA_VERSION = 18`), `src/domain/record-schemas.ts` (`customValuesSchema`, the task's map extracted unchanged; `customValues` optional as the last key of the eight kinds; `systemFieldKeys`), `src/domain/records.ts` (re-exports `systemFieldKeys`), `src/domain/config-defaults.ts` (`defaultKindSchema` from `systemFieldKeys`), `src/components/settings/roles-tab.tsx` (field-rule rows from `systemFieldKeys`), `src/app/api/openapi.json/route.ts` (resource schemas from the system field keys), `src/lib/mcp/tools.ts` (the create and update inputs of job roles, vacancies, applications, interviews, offers, onboarding and checklist items omit `customValues`; `factsOf` uses `systemFieldKeys`), `src/lib/bulk-intake.ts` (bulk items refuse `customValues`), `src/lib/authz.ts` (`hiddenKeys` and the `authorize` field map fail closed: a non-task `customValues` is hidden from a role that hides any field of the kind and names no rule for `customValues`), `src/lib/config-store.ts` (`currentConfigBody` and `kindSchemaFor` take the access state's documents and throw without them), `src/lib/records.ts` (`kindSchemaPin` written in the insert of `createInside` and the data update of `updateRecord`), `docs/api-examples.md` ("Custom values on records"), `docs/deployment.md` (the `0040` paragraph), `docs/security.md` (the fail-closed rule), this entry.
- **Tests:** `tests/migration-upgrade.test.ts` (new "upgrade from 0039 to record pins": a fictional baseline with all 13 kinds, a task with a task-field value, an archived record, a private view, a comment, a private state and three audit rows at fixed ids and times; every earlier column unchanged, both pins null, marker 18, a second run of `0040` changes nothing, and every default list and get body equals byte for byte `tests/fixtures/record-pins-rest-baseline.json`, which `main`'s code wrote in the branch's first commit; earlier cases change only marker 17 to 18 and leave out the two columns), `tests/records-postgres.test.ts` (new describe on its own database: the default pin per configurable kind, null for the other four kinds and every `type_id`; a published job-role kind schema pins a create and re-pins an update of an earlier record; a row inserted by SQL reads byte for byte the same before and after an unrelated write and gains no `customValues` when updated; custom values round-trip, unchecked, with the bounded refusals answering 400 `invalid_input`; pins sent as input are refused; no response names a pin), `tests/records-domain.test.ts` (the shape per kind, absent means no key, task default `{}`, `systemFieldKeys`), `tests/config-defaults.test.ts` (`TODAY` from the keys without the non-task `customValues`, so it still equals `RESOURCE_CONFIG`; the interviewer template hides candidate `customValues`), `tests/openapi-contract.test.ts` (only the task schema has `customValues`; no pin name in the document), `tests/mcp-candidate-task-tools.test.ts` (only `create_task` and `update_task` take `customValues`; `get_candidate` returns none; `add_bulk_import_item` refuses them), `tests/bulk-intake.test.ts` (a REST bulk item with `customValues` answers 400 `invalid_input`), `tests/authz.test.ts` (one new case: the interviewer template as published before the key existed hides candidate `customValues` in `hiddenKeys`, `redact` and the `authorize` field map, an explicit `read` rule shows them, tasks keep their per-field rules; two earlier expectations now include `customValues` in the hidden set of a role with hidden rules), a records-postgres case (a role published with the earlier interviewer field rules gets no custom values from a get or list and finds nothing by searching for one, while the owner does), `tests/cv-storage-migration.test.ts`, `tests/connection-boundary-boot.test.ts` and `tests/db-pool-preflight.test.ts` (marker 18). `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` (6, 11 and 15) and `tests/runtime-role-grants.test.ts` are unchanged and pass.
- **Local:** done with Node 24 on PGlite with fictional data. `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm test` (107 files, 1,637 tests), `npm run test:storage`, `npm run build` and Prettier on every changed file pass in the worktree. The OpenAPI document, the MCP `tools/list` inputs, `RESOURCE_CONFIG` and `DEFAULT_KIND_SCHEMAS` generated by the branch are byte-identical to `main`'s; the role templates differ only by the interviewer's hidden candidate `customValues`.
- **Owner steps:** apply `0040` after `0039` on Preview; the role template is unchanged and needs no rerun. Hosted startup refuses with 503 `storage_schema_mismatch` until it is applied. Open for review (the spec's assumptions): the value shape is exactly the task union with no key-count cap beyond the 256 KiB body limit (1); the four non-configurable kinds keep a null pin (2); only a create and the data update re-pin, never the bucket-archive task move, the scope trigger or erasure (3); an invalid published kind schema makes writes of that kind fail rather than pin the default, unreachable today since no route publishes kind schemas (4); OpenAPI does not document the non-task `customValues` REST now accepts, while `docs/api-examples.md` does, an API-contract choice the approver may escalate (5); MCP neither takes nor returns non-task custom values and bulk intake refuses them, for R2b-04 to revisit (6); the interviewer template now hides candidate `customValues`, so a role copied from it shows one more rule in the roles tab count; interviewer and other roles published before this change carry no such rule, so the service fails closed instead: a role that hides any field of a non-task kind also hides that kind's `customValues` unless it names a rule for them, which keeps an earlier interviewer at name and job title (7, changed after the security review); no foreign key or index on the pins, since the default ids are not `app.config_versions` rows (8); the before responses are a golden file written by `main`'s code (9).

### R2b-03 Field type engine (job 068)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item R2b-03 (milestone M5 "Fields and screens", epic R2-02, spec IDs FLD-010, FLD-011 and FLD-013; architecture [§1.5](../release-2/architecture.md#15-dynamic-validation-with-the-strict-schemas-authoritative) item 4, [§1.2](../release-2/architecture.md#12-where-configuration-lives) bounds and [§12](../release-2/architecture.md#12-performance-and-scale-targets)). The new pure module `src/domain/field-types.ts` defines the R2-02 data types as a fixed set with no registry or plug-in (`FIELD_TYPES`), a strict custom field definition per type (`customFieldSchema`: uuid key, cleaned label, help, required, and only the min, max, pattern, options, currency, target and default its type lists), a bounded value schema per definition that never rewrites a value (`fieldValueSchema`), a static pattern check refused before publish (`patternIssue`: 1 to 200 characters, compiles under `u`, no backreference, lookaround, or repeated group holding a quantifier or alternation, and at most one choice point in the whole pattern: one quantifier whose count varies or one `|`), the task field type map (`TASK_FIELD_TYPE_MAP`) and `compileKindSchema`, which gives one strict `customValues` validator and the mandatory fields per screen action, cached by version id (512 entries, least recently used first). `kindSchemaBodySchema` now accepts custom fields beside the system fields. FLD-013 holds because no type change can be expressed. Not wired into create, update, REST, OpenAPI or MCP (R2b-04). No row status changes. **Count:** the build plan and architecture §1.5 and §14.6 say "19 types", but R2-02 in `docs/ROADMAP.md` and the list in §1.5 itself name 18; exactly those 18 were built and no 19th was invented.
- **Files touched:** `src/domain/field-types.ts` (new; it also now holds `configLabel`, `configHelpText`, `configOptionValue` and `CONFIGURABLE_KINDS`, since it cannot import `config.ts` at load), `src/domain/config.ts` (re-exports those four under the same names; `fields` is a union of system and custom fields told apart by `system`; the "not a field of the kind" and select-options rules apply to system fields only; key uniqueness and screens cover both), `src/domain/config-defaults.ts` (type only: a default body holds system fields only, so `RESOURCE_CONFIG` compiles unchanged), this entry.
- **Tests:** `tests/field-types.test.ts` (new, no database, fictional data): the 18 types in order; every type's valid, empty, wrong-type and out-of-bound values, each valid output equal to its input and inside today's stored union except multi-select; every definition refusal (inapplicable attribute, 121-character label, 501-character help, 201-character option, duplicate option ids and labels, `min` above `max`, a default outside its own schema, a default on person, org unit, file or lookup, money without a currency) and cleaning of padded, zero-width and bidi text; the pattern catalogue, a body refused at `["fields", <index>, "pattern"]`, a refused pattern never compiled or run against a default, the chained-quantifier refusals (`a*a*a*b`, `.?` × 60, `w?d?` × 30, `(?:a|aa)` × 24), an accepted worst case (`.*.{3000}!`) refusing a 4000-character value within 50 ms, and an over-long or too-short value on a patterned field reporting only its length issue; the task map against a copy of `validCustomValue`; compile output, freezing, the cache, eviction and load order; and the largest configuration of §12 (150 fields, 120 custom fields across all 18 types with 100 options, 120-character labels, 500-character help and 200-character patterns, three screens of 150 fields, under 512 KiB) passing the body schema and compiling in a median of at most 20 ms over 5 cold compiles. `tests/config-defaults.test.ts`, `tests/config-store.test.ts`, `tests/records-domain.test.ts` and `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` (6, 11 and 15) pass unchanged.
- **Local:** done with Node 24 and fictional data, no migration. `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm test` (108 files, 1,647 tests), `npm run test:storage`, `npm run build` and Prettier on every changed file pass in the worktree. Compiling the largest configuration takes about 4 ms. After the security review, the worst accepted patterns measured on a 4000-character value took 16 to 23 ms (for example `.*.{3000}!` and `p{L}*p{L}{2000}!`), where two choice points with a long fixed tail took 623 ms and the reviewer's chained patterns up to 96 s.
- **Owner steps:** none to deploy. Open for review (the spec's assumptions): multi-select values are an array of option ids, outside today's scalar `customValuesSchema` union, so R2b-04 must widen the stored union before they can be saved (2); money stores the offer amount's decimal string with a required per-field ISO currency (3); 40 screens and 30 types per kind cannot be expressed until R2b-07 and R2b-11, so the largest-configuration test uses the 150 fields and three screens a body can hold today (11); person, org unit, file and lookup take no default, since configuration may not hold record ids and their reference checks come in R2b-05 and R2b-06 (6); option ids are cleaned strings rather than uuids, so R2b-04 can map today's task options as id = label (7); the pattern check is static and conservative and refuses some safe patterns such as `(ab|cd)+` (9). The one-choice-point rule goes beyond the spec's refusal list, added after the security review because chained quantifiers outside a repeated group (`a*a*a*b`) backtrack for seconds on a 4000-character value: it keeps every safe pattern of the spec's catalogue, but also refuses useful ones such as `[A-Z]{2,3}-d+`, `p{L}{2,}(?:-p{L}+)?` and `a|b|c`. Text length checks abort, so a pattern never runs on a value over its bound.

### ADM-02 Teams list and Create team (job 069)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item ADM-02 (backlog `R2-ADM-02`, milestone M1 "Admin shell and Teams move", epic R2-01; spec IDs IAM-053, DOM-004 and TST-005; architecture [§14.1](../release-2/architecture.md#141-admin-shell-and-sitemap-under-appadmin) and [§14.2](../release-2/architecture.md#142-teams-moves-into-the-admin-portal); owner decisions D1 and D20, option A of 10 November 2026). `/app/admin/teams` (table key `teams`, served by `[tab]/page.tsx`) lists the teams where the caller is team owner or admin; company owners and company admins also see every other team of the workspace by name, identifier and created date, marked "Not a member", with no control and no id, members, counts, records, storage or activity (TST-005). The active team's row opens `/app/admin/members`; another owned or administered team has a button that switches the active team as Team & access does and then opens it, until ADM-03 adds team detail. **Create a team** moves into one shared component shown on the Teams page and still in Team & access, only when `canCreateTeam`, with the same Better Auth create, `app.map_new_team()` mapping and no company authority for the creator. Teams is the one page table level that both a team role and company authority open. No grantable area, redirect or Team & access removal (ADM-03, ADM-04); no row status changes.
- **Files touched:** `drizzle/0041_workspace_team_list.sql` (new: `tenant_context.workspace_team_list()`, owner-run `SECURITY DEFINER`, session company owners and admins only, the context workspace's teams as `(id, name, slug, created_at)`; storage schema 19), `infrastructure/postgres-split-runtime-roles.sql` (one data-role `EXECUTE` grant), `src/db/connection-boundary.ts` (the helper in `dataRoleHelpers` after `role_assignment_changes`), `src/db/storage-schema.ts`, `src/lib/admin-teams.ts` (new: `listAdminTeams`, 403 `teams_forbidden`), `src/app/api/v1/teams/route.ts` (new: `GET /api/v1/teams`, session only, no query keys), `src/app/api/openapi.json/route.ts`, `src/components/settings/admin-tabs.ts` (the `teams` row first in People and the `teams` level), `src/components/settings/admin-portal.tsx`, `src/components/settings/teams-tab.tsx` (new: `TeamsTab` and the pure `TeamsPanel`), `src/components/settings/create-team.tsx` (new: `CreateTeam`, moved from `team-settings.tsx` with its texts, plus `ensure`), `src/components/settings/team-settings.tsx` (renders `CreateTeam`), `scripts/live-role-tests.ts` (`workspace-team-list-team-b-owner-denied`), `docs/user-guide.md`, `docs/identity-boundary-plan.md`, `docs/deployment.md` (the `0041` paragraph), `docs/accessibility-checklist.md` and this entry. `src/lib/auth-policy.ts`, `src/lib/auth.ts`, `src/domain/config.ts`, `0028` and `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` are unchanged.
- **Tests:** `tests/admin-teams.test.ts` (new, PGlite split roles, fictional data: the helper's four columns for the workspace's teams to a company owner and a company admin; `42501` for a team-only owner, a recruiter, a viewer and a token context of the company owner; a team moved by the owner role into a second, owner-inserted workspace never appears, and that workspace's own company owner sees only it; `GET /api/v1/teams` rows with exactly six keys, a team-only owner's own owner teams only, every workspace team for a company owner and admin with `teamId` and `teamRole` null on other teams and no member name, email, user id or other team id in the body; 403 `teams_forbidden` for a recruiter and a viewer, 403 `session_required` for a Bearer token, 401 anonymous, 400 for a query key; through Better Auth's `organization/create` a team owner's new team is mapped in the workspace, listed as Owner and adds no `app.workspace_admins` row, and a recruiter is refused). `tests/admin-portal-ui.test.tsx` (ten pages with Teams first in People; both `adminTabs()` matrices give `teams` for a team owner or admin or any company level; the Teams card and sidebar link; the panel's Open link and Switch button only on owner and admin rows, text-only Not a member, Recruiter and Viewer rows, the busy, loading and error states; Create only with `canCreateTeam`). `tests/admin-routes.test.ts` (Teams for team owners and admins, both company admins and both company owners; a new viewer caller with none). `tests/runtime-role-grants.test.ts` and `tests/connection-boundary.test.ts` (0041 blocks), `tests/openapi-contract.test.ts`, and the marker 19 in `tests/cv-storage-migration.test.ts`, `tests/connection-boundary-boot.test.ts`, `tests/db-pool-preflight.test.ts` and `tests/migration-upgrade.test.ts`. `tests/settings-ui.test.tsx` and `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` pass unchanged.
