The cluster was then stopped and deleted. The pooled run was not done: no PgBouncer binary was available and none may be downloaded. In the browser as the fictional seed owner at 1280×800, 768×1024 and 360×740 in both themes: the Teams card and link, the list with Open and Switch, creating fictional teams from Admin > Teams and from Team & access; see the accessibility checklist.
- **Owner steps:** apply `0041` after `0040` on Preview, rerun the role template, then `scripts/db-role-evidence.ts` (zero drift) and `npm run test:live-roles`, direct and through the pooler. Until `0041` is applied, hosted startup refuses with 503 `storage_schema_mismatch`; until the template is rerun, a company owner's or admin's Teams list answers 403 `teams_forbidden`. Open for review (the spec's assumptions): Teams is served by `[tab]/page.tsx`, with no new page file (1); a new table level `teams` is the one page both a team role and company authority open (2); access follows the active team's role, so an owner of another team who is a recruiter in the active one switches first (3); until ADM-03 the active team's row opens Members and another managed team's row switches teams first (4); a company owner or admin who is a recruiter or viewer in a team sees that role, not "Not a member", with no control (5); the API gives no team id on a row where the caller is not a member (6); Create a team is shared, not moved, until ADM-04 (7); the helper is session only (8); no archived state until ADM-05 (9); rows sorted by name then identifier, no paging (10); the refusal code is 403 `teams_forbidden` and the page shows the plain no-access state (11); `scripts/runtime-role-template.ts` needs no edit, since it parses the template (12); Teams is first in People (13).

### ADM-06 Re-authentication for sensitive admin changes (job 070)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item ADM-06 (backlog `R2-ADM-06`, milestone M2 "Users and groups", epic R2-13; spec IDs IAM-035, UI-004 and PRM-013; architecture §14.14 "Re-authentication" and §14.15, threat T-R2-30; owner decision D29, option A of 10 November 2026: no policy page, sidebar entry or setting). One server check, `requireFreshSession()` in `src/lib/fresh-session.ts`, refuses a session as old as Better Auth's `freshAge` (15 minutes, `>=`, measured from its creation) or without a start time with 403 `SESSION_NOT_FRESH`, with no database statement. `runApi`'s new `freshSession` option runs it after `requirePrincipal` and before the handler, on exactly four `POST` routes: role publish, configured role assignment and clearing, company authority changes and a member's two-step reset. They stay session only, a Bearer token is refused with 403 `session_required` before any token lookup, MCP has no tool for them, and `authorize()`, the services and the `tenant_context` helpers are unchanged. No `GET` route checks freshness. On the refusal, the Roles & permissions page and Team & access open **Confirm it's you**, re-authenticate through Better Auth's `signIn.email`, `twoFactor.verifyTotp` (never trusting the device) and `organization.setActive`, keep the form's state and retry the change once. SRS-ID-06 gains the check, the dialog and the new test; no row status changes.
- **Files touched:** `src/lib/auth-policy.ts` (`SESSION_FRESH_AGE_SECONDS`), `src/lib/auth.ts` (`freshAge` from it), `src/lib/fresh-session.ts` (new), `src/lib/principal.ts` (`sessionCreatedAt` on the cookie path), `src/lib/api.ts` (the `freshSession` option), `src/app/api/v1/roles/[id]/publish/route.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/teams/member-roles/route.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/company-authority/route.ts` and `src/app/api/v1/teams/two-step-reset/route.ts` (`freshSession: true` on `POST`), `src/app/api/openapi.json/route.ts` (the recent sign-in sentence on the four operations), `src/components/settings/fresh-session-dialog.tsx` (new: `retryWithFreshSession`, `reauthenticate`, `FreshSessionDialog` and `useFreshSession`), `src/components/settings/roles-tab.tsx` and `src/components/settings/team-settings.tsx` (the four requests wrapped with `withFreshSession`; a cancelled re-authentication ends the action with no notice or error), `docs/authentication.md`, `docs/user-guide.md`, `docs/accessibility-checklist.md` and this entry.

- **Local:** done with Node 24 on PGlite with fictional data. `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm test` (110 files, 1,682 tests), `npm run test:storage`, `npm run build` and Prettier on every changed file pass in the worktree. In the browser as the fictional seed owner with a session set 16 minutes back: viewing never asked; company authority, role publish and configured role assignment opened the dialog, Cancel and Escape kept the form, a wrong password or code saved nothing, the right password and code saved once in the same team, and a second save within 15 minutes needed no dialog; 360 px and the dark theme checked. See the accessibility checklist.
- **Owner steps:** none beyond deploying: no migration, role template or environment change. Open for review (the spec's assumptions): the code is checked only when Better Auth challenges for it, so a device trusted at sign-in re-authenticates with the password alone (1); cancelling after the password was accepted but before the code leaves the browser signed out, and the page says so (2); the previous session row is left in place (3); "member role assignment" is the configured-role route, not Better Auth team-role changes (4); the code field shows only for users with two-step sign-in on (5); the code is the upper-case `SESSION_NOT_FRESH` (6); the freshness check runs before the service's authorization (7); only the authenticator code is accepted in the dialog, not a backup code (8).

### ADM-22 Org tree by units (job 071)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item ADM-22 (backlog `R2-ADM-22`, milestone M4 "Org tree and job architecture", epic R2-14; spec ID ORG-101; architecture [§14.5](../release-2/architecture.md#145-org-tree-and-job-architecture-org-101-to-org-107); the owner's mockup change of 10 November 2026, D13 and D16). Organisation > Org chart gains a **List | Org tree** switch; List stays the default and unchanged. **Org tree** is a read-only nested list (`role="tree"`, treeitems with level, set size and position, roving tab stop and the WAI-ARIA tree keys) over the same chart state, drawn top-down above 720 px and as an indented outline at 720 px and narrower, with Expand all (breadth first, at most 4 units loading at once, never through Show more, stopping at 500 units with a message), Collapse all, zoom from 50% to 150%, and a PNG export drawn on a canvas in the browser. Every position response gains `vacant`, built in the same statement: true or false for a context the directory view rule allows, null otherwise. No route, query key, table, migration, RLS policy, MCP tool or access rule changes; no row status changes.

In the browser as the fictional seed owner at 1280×900 and 360×800 in both themes: the switch, expanding one unit (two requests, none on the second expand), every tree key, Collapse all moving the tab stop, Expand all, zoom bounds, the export (no request) and the outline at 360 px; see the accessibility checklist.

Open for review (the spec's assumptions): vacancy comes from a new `vacant` key on the existing position responses, computed in the same statement (1); `vacant` is null without directory view, so a company admin with only the organisation area sees no vacancy (2); a current employee is an active, non-archived employee with a current position row, so a position held only by someone who left shows Vacant while List > Show employees still lists them (3); `vacant` is on POST and PATCH responses too (4); the unit tree shows no names, not even the manager's (5); List stays the default and the choice is not remembered (6); the 500 units count every unit in the loaded tree, and Expand all does not page through Show more (7); at most 4 units load at once during Expand all (8); Collapse all is included (9); the tree is an indented outline at 720 px and narrower (10); the exported image is always light and at 100% (11); this heading follows the existing entries (12).

### ADM-03 Team detail: Members and Settings (job 072)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item ADM-03 (backlog `R2-ADM-03`, milestone M1 "Admin shell and Teams move", epic R2-01; spec IDs IAM-053, IAM-070 and IAM-071; architecture [§14.1](../release-2/architecture.md#141-admin-shell-and-sitemap-under-appadmin) and [§14.2](../release-2/architecture.md#142-teams-moves-into-the-admin-portal); owner decisions D1, D20 and D29, option A of 8 and 10 November 2026). The new page `/app/admin/teams/[team]` (`[team]` is the team id) opens only for an owner or admin of that team: one server check, `teamPageAccess()` beside `adminPageAccess()`, reads the session user's membership of `[team]` and runs one `withTeam` as that member, through `authorize()` (`meAccess()`), before anything renders. A company owner or company admin who is not a member, for any id, gets only "You are not a member of this team" (`NOT_A_MEMBER`) with no sidebar and no team data, since company authority never opens team data (TST-005); everyone else gets "You do not have access to this page". The allowed page shows the Admin portal shell and the active team's sidebar with Teams marked, the team's name and an ARIA tablist with **Members** and **Settings** (`?tab=settings`), and never switches the active team. Members is Team & access's member management, moved into one shared `TeamMembers` component that both pages render, with the same calls, confirmations, messages and the job 070 fresh-session retry; every request names the page's team (Better Auth `organizationId`, `X-Team-Id` through `requestJson`). Settings renames the team through the same Better Auth update. Admin > Teams now links every owned or administered team to its page with **Open**; **Switch and open** is gone. Team & access, the Admin `members`, `cv-storage`, `speed`, `tokens` and `task-boards` pages and the page table are unchanged (ADM-04). No API route, migration, grantable area, MCP or role template change; no row status changes.
- **Files touched:** `src/app/app/admin/teams/[team]/page.tsx` (new), `src/lib/admin-access.ts` (`teamPageAccess`, sharing one `sessionAccess` with `adminPageAccess`), `src/lib/workspace.ts` (`getTeamWorkspaceContext`: `getWorkspaceContext` plus the user's membership of one team, from the same membership query), `src/components/settings/team-members.tsx` (new: `TeamMembers`, `MembersTable`, `teamRequests`, and `MemberActions`, `MemberRoleControl`, `ConfiguredRolesNotice`, `canResetTwoStep`, `canAssignRoles` moved from `team-settings.tsx`), `src/components/settings/team-settings.tsx` (renders `TeamMembers` for the active team; keeps Your teams, rename, Prepare task board, CV storage and Create a team), `src/components/settings/team-detail.tsx` (new: `TeamDetailPortal`, `TeamDetail` and the rename form), `src/components/settings/admin-portal.tsx` (`AdminPortalView` takes `refusal` and `detail`), `src/components/settings/admin-tabs.ts` (`NOT_A_MEMBER`), `src/components/settings/teams-tab.tsx` (Open links to the team's page; no switch), `src/components/ui/tab-keys.ts` (new: `tabKeys`, moved from `records-screen.tsx`), `src/components/workspace/records-screen.tsx` (imports it), `docs/user-guide.md`, `docs/accessibility-checklist.md` and this entry. `drizzle/`, `infrastructure/`, `src/app/api/`, `src/lib/mcp/`, `src/lib/auth.ts`, `src/lib/principal.ts`, `src/lib/authz.ts` and `package.json` are unchanged.
- **Tests:** `tests/admin-routes.test.ts` (the new page file maps to `teams`; the page-table matrix passes `{ team }` and expects the detail outcome on team A for every caller; a new team detail matrix on split-role PGlite with fictional data: sign-in for anonymous and a Bearer token with no token lookup; allowed for a team owner, a team admin and a company owner who is team owner; refused for an owner of another team only, a recruiter, a viewer, a company owner who is a recruiter there and a user with no team; "You are not a member of this team" for a company admin without and with the organisation area, a company owner outside a third team and a company owner on a made-up id, which is refused for a team-only owner; each with no team name, member name or email in the markup and one `withTeam`, in `[team]` for a member and in the active team otherwise). `tests/admin-portal-ui.test.tsx` (the Teams panel's Open links to `/app/admin/teams/<teamId>` on every owner and admin row with no button; the detail's shell, heading, tablist with exactly Members and Settings and their ARIA attributes, Settings by `?tab=settings` and Members otherwise, the rename form, the members table with "(you)", Last owner and Reset two-step sign-in, the Configured role column only for a company owner, the detail shown when the active team lists no page, the not-a-member markup, and a fake Better Auth client and fake `fetch` showing every Members and Settings request, and the retry after Confirm it's you, naming the page's team, not the active one). `tests/settings-ui.test.tsx` (the member cases import from `team-members.tsx`; assertions unchanged). `tests/auth-flows.test.ts`, `tests/fresh-session.test.ts`, `tests/member-roles.test.ts`, `tests/two-step-reset.test.ts`, `tests/admin-teams.test.ts` and `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` (6, 11 and 15) pass unchanged.
- **Local:** done with Node 24 on PGlite with fictional data, no migration. `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm test` (111 files, 1,711 tests), `npm run test:storage`, `npm run build` (its route list has `/app/admin/teams/[team]`; `/app/admin/teams` is still served by `[tab]/page.tsx`, so no `teams/page.tsx` was needed) and Prettier on every changed file pass in the worktree. In the browser as the fictional seed owner with another team current: Open on both teams, the detail of a non-current team with its own members, invite and cancel, a team role change, a configured role with a stale session (Cancel, then password and code, saved once), the rename, Team & access unchanged, at 1280×800, 768×1024 and 360×740 in both themes; see the accessibility checklist.
- **Owner steps:** none: no migration or setting. Open for review (the spec's assumptions): `[team]` is the team id (1); the page works for any team the caller owns or administers, not only the current one, so the Teams list links instead of switching (2); the sidebar stays the current team's (3); a non-member company owner or admin gets the not-a-member state for any id, existing or not (4); a company owner or admin who is a recruiter or viewer in the team is refused, not told they are not a member (5); no page table row, sidebar entry or Admin home card for the detail (6); the tab is read from `?tab=` on load and switching does not change the address (7); Prepare task board stays in Team & access (8); removing yourself goes to `/app/settings/team` (9); the not-a-member state has no sidebar (10). Built this way: member notices on Team & access now show above the Members panel, member actions no longer disable the rest of that page while they run, and its Your teams **Refresh** reloads the team list only (the member list reloads after each change), because the member list is one component with its own state; and the team detail's Settings tab has its own rename form, as Team & access keeps its own until ADM-04.

### ADM-07 Users list and the people area (job 073)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item ADM-07 (backlog `R2-ADM-07`, milestone M2 "Users and groups", epic R2-13; spec IDs IAM-005 and IAM-006; architecture [§14.3](../release-2/architecture.md#143-users-and-groups-as-company-objects) and [§14.14](../release-2/architecture.md#1414-company-areas-for-the-new-pages); owner decisions D1 and D20, option A of 8 and 10 November 2026). The new grantable company area `people` (`GRANTABLE_ADMIN_AREAS`, in the database's order) is offered in Company authority with a plain description under its checkbox. `/app/admin/users` (table key `users`, first in People, served by `[tab]/page.tsx`, level `people`: company owners and company admins holding `people`) lists, 25 at a time with **Show more**, everyone with a membership in a team of the workspace (Active) and every pending, unexpired invitation to one of them for an email that is not a member's (Invited): name, email, teams with team role and configured role, company level, status, last sign-in (the newest stored session's start, "Not recorded" when none) and two-step, filtered by team, status, company level and configured role, with plain loading, error, refused and empty states. The owner-run helper `tenant_context.workspace_people` returns no team id, record, candidate, upload, storage, activity, token or session data and nothing outside the context's workspace; the session-only `GET /api/v1/admin/users` (403 `session_required` for tokens, 403 `people_forbidden` for everyone `authorize()` or the helper refuses) serves the page. No user detail page, invite, deactivate, group, export or bulk control (ADM-08 onwards); rows have no control.

`42501` and 403 `people_forbidden` for a company admin with `fields` only, a team-only owner, a recruiter and a viewer, `42501` for a token context of the company owner, 403 `session_required` for a Bearer token and 400 `invalid_input` for an unknown key, bad values and malformed cursors, each with no data-database call; The cluster was then stopped and deleted. The pooled run was not done: no PgBouncer binary was available and none may be downloaded. In the browser as the fictional seed owner and a fictional member at 1280×800, 768×1024 and 360×740 in both themes: the Users card and link, the list, filters, Show more and its focus, invited rows, the People checkbox, and the member's access with People and with Organisation only; see the accessibility checklist.

Open for review (the spec's assumptions): status is Active or Invited until ADM-09 (1); invited rows have no user id, name, level or last sign-in and leave out a member's email (2); last sign-in is the newest stored session's start, "Not recorded" when none (3); the route is `GET /api/v1/admin/users` (4); filters use the team slug and the role key (5); the role choices come back with the list (6); a suspended authority row gives no company level (7); the helper returns 26 rows and the page shows 25 (8); filters apply with **Apply filters**, ordered by name (email for invited rows) (9); Users is first in People (10); `scripts/runtime-role-template.ts` needed no edit (11); no re-authentication to view (12). Built this way: the count line says "Showing 1 person" for one row; the table uses the Directory's `compact` style so its seven columns fit at 1280 px, and the record screens' phone layout (`records-panel`) so it stacks at 360 px, which the other admin tables do not; and a `level=none` filter also keeps invited rows, which hold no level.

### ADM-23 Org tree by people (job 074)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item ADM-23 (backlog `R2-ADM-23`, milestone M4 "Org tree and job architecture", epic R2-14; spec ID ORG-101; architecture [§14.5](../release-2/architecture.md#145-org-tree-and-job-architecture-org-101-to-org-107); depends on ADM-22; owner decisions of 8 and 10 November 2026: the org tree by units and by people, reporting lines derived from `is_manager` positions with no new data, D13 and D16). With the directory page and directory view (`peopleViewAllowed`), Organisation > Org chart > Org tree gains an **Org tree shows: By units | By people** switch; By units stays the default and unchanged, and without that access there is no switch, no people text and no request. **By people** is a read-only nested list (`role="tree"` "Org tree by people", person treeitems with level, set size, position and `aria-expanded` only with reports, roving tab stop of its own, the WAI-ARIA tree keys) loaded one level at a time from the new session-only `GET /api/v1/employees/reporting-lines` (roots, or `managerId`'s reports, 100 a page with Show more), drawn top-down above 720 px and as an indented outline at 720 px and narrower, with Expand all (breadth first, only people with reports, at most 4 loads at once, never through Show more, stopping at 500 people), Collapse all, the shared zoom and a PNG export drawn on a canvas in the browser. The route derives every reporting line in one statement (own unit: the manager position nearest the top, else the primary position, else the first by title; manager: the first holder by name of a manager position in the nearest unit at or above it with a current holder, skipping units the person manages), checks `authorize(…, { page: "directory", resource: "directory", action: "view" })` first, reads under `allows_directory` RLS and returns exactly `{id, displayName, title, unitName, otherPositions, reports}` under either directory rule. No table, column, index, migration, RLS policy, `tenant_context` function, role template, MCP tool or existing response changes; no row status changes.
- **Files touched:** `src/domain/directory.ts` (`reportingLinesQuery`), `src/lib/directory.ts` (`ReportingPerson`, `listReportingLines`; `employeeJson`, `positionJson`, `listEmployees`, `listPositions` and `directoryView` unchanged), `src/app/api/v1/employees/reporting-lines/route.ts` (new: `GET`, `humanOnly`), `src/app/api/openapi.json/route.ts` (the route), `src/components/settings/org-tree.ts` (`PersonNode`, `buildPeopleTree`, `peopleCount`, `peopleCountsText`, `personDetails`, `PeopleExpandStatus`, `peopleExpandMessage`, `expandAllPeople`; `treeKey` with `togglePerson`, `treeLayout` and `drawLayout` placing person boxes, `LAYOUT.person`, `imageFilename(now, "people")`; every units export keeps its signature and output), `src/components/settings/organisation-tab.tsx` (`TreeMode`, `PeopleState`, `PeopleHandlers`, `EMPTY_PEOPLE`, `peopleViewAllowed`, `reportingPath`, `loadReports`, the people state and handlers in `OrganisationTab`, the switch and rule line in `OrgChartPanel`, `PeopleTreeView`, `PersonTreeItem`, the toolbar shared as `TreeToolbar` with the same markup, the export shared as `saveImage`), `docs/user-guide.md`, `docs/accessibility-checklist.md`, `docs/security.md`, `docs/api-examples.md` and this entry. `src/app/globals.css` needed no change. `admin-tabs.ts`, `src/app/app/admin/**`, `drizzle/`, `infrastructure/`, `src/lib/mcp/`, `src/lib/authz.ts`, the existing employee, position and org-unit routes, `package.json` and `package-lock.json` are unchanged.
- **Tests:** `tests/directory.test.ts` (new cases only, fictional data: a new describe with its own `createTestDb()` for the rule: a person reports to their unit's manager and that manager to the one above; a manager position with no holder, only a left or archived holder or only an ended row skips to the nearest unit above with a holder, also two units up; the top manager and a tree with no manager position are roots; a person with a primary non-manager position and another elsewhere reports through the primary one with `otherPositions` 1, a person with manager positions in two units reports from the one nearest the top though the other is primary, and the crossed pair appears once each with no loop, crawled from the roots; two holders of one manager position give the first by name; left, archived, ended and position-less employees are in no level and their `managerId` gives an empty page; `reports` equals the rows of each level, order by name and `hasMore` paging; 404 for an unknown manager, 400 for an unknown key and bad values. In the split-role harness: a company owner (full) and a configured admin-membership role with `directoryRule` basic get rows with exactly the six keys, and no seeded work email, phone or employee number in any response; a recruiter, a company admin with the organisation area whose team role has no directory view and a configured role with directory view but without the `directory` page get 403 `directory_forbidden` with no `data`; a Bearer token gets 403 `session_required`; at most 6 round trips. A scale describe with its own `createTestDb()`: 2,000 fictional employees on 420 units across 6 levels, a manager position in 9 units of 10, one in 6 of them vacant, every 25th person holding two positions). `tests/org-tree.test.ts` (new cases only: the people tree's order, levels, set sizes, paths, labels with counts, `expanded` only with reports, a collapsed person hiding what was loaded, the Show more items, loading and could-not-load; the people counts text; the layout placing every expanded person's reports below them and none for a collapsed person with no overlap; `imageFilename` for people; people Expand all with a fake requester whose every person has 100 reports stops at 402 with the limit message, at most 4 requests in flight, only `/api/v1/employees/reporting-lines?managerId=…&page=1&pageSize=100` paths and never a person with no reports; a small tree finishes with its count; the first error stops it and names the person). `tests/organisation-ui.test.tsx` (new cases only: the switch with By units pressed by default when allowed and absent, with no people text, when not; `peopleViewAllowed`; the request paths; the people tree's roles, aria attributes, own tab stop and no controls; no email, phone, number or user in the markup even when rows carry them; the loading, error, empty, person loading and error, Show more busy and export texts; the Expand all messages; `treeKey` for every key on person nodes and the tab stop after a collapse; the zoom bounds in people mode). `tests/openapi-contract.test.ts` gains the route in its session-only list (one added line). `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` (6, 11 and 15) and the existing cases of the org chart, scope and route suites pass unchanged.
- **Local:** done with Node 24 on PGlite with fictional data, no migration. One level of the 2,000-employee fixture, after one warm-up request, measured with `performance.now()` around the route call: the roots 40 ms, a top manager's level 44 ms and a mid-tree manager's level 40 ms, each in 6 round trips. `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm test` (112 files, 1,762 tests), `npm run test:storage`, `npm run build` and Prettier on every changed file pass in the worktree, and `npm run bench:round-trips` prints the same round trips on all 31 rows as on `main` (run from a detached `main` worktree on its own local PGlite). In the browser as the fictional seed owner at 1280×900 and 360×800 in both themes: the switch, one request per expand and none on a second, every key, Collapse all moving the tab stop, Expand all, zoom bounds, the export (no request) and the outline at 360 px; see the accessibility checklist.
- **Owner steps:** none: no migration. Open for review (the spec's assumptions): a new read-only route serves one level, since the existing routes cannot (1); a person's own unit is that of their manager position nearest the top, else their primary position, else their first by title, so no line loops (2); with several holders of a unit's manager positions the first by display name is the manager (3); only active, non-archived employees with a current position are in the tree (4); rows carry name, title, unit and two counts under both directory rules (5); visibility and the route both need the directory page and directory view (6); the rule cases are in `tests/directory.test.ts` (7); the 500 limit counts loaded people (8); By units stays the default, the choice is not remembered and zoom is shared (9); Expand all and Collapse all are offered in people mode (10); the 2,000-employee timing is a vitest case after one warm-up request; if CI machines are slower and it becomes flaky, that is for the owner to decide (11); this heading follows the existing entries (12). Built this way: the statement walks the units through two jsonb maps (each unit's ancestors, each unit's manager holders) rather than joins, because PGlite has no table statistics and a join plan took minutes on the 2,000-employee fixture; the toolbar has no Export image until the top level has people, as in the tree by units; and a person's box shows "Loading the people reporting to <name>…" but no error line of its own, the error being the alert next to the tree.

### ADM-04 Team detail: CV storage, Speed, Task boards and Activity; redirects; personal settings (job 075)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item ADM-04 (backlog `R2-ADM-04`, milestone M1 "Admin shell and Teams move", epic R2-01; spec IDs IAM-053, IMP-001 and AUD-004; architecture [§14.1](../release-2/architecture.md#141-admin-shell-and-sitemap-under-appadmin) and [§14.2](../release-2/architecture.md#142-teams-moves-into-the-admin-portal); depends on ADM-03; owner decisions D1, D18, D20 and D33, option A of 8 and 10 November 2026). The team detail page `/app/admin/teams/[team]` now has six tabs, **Members**, **Settings**, **CV storage**, **Speed**, **Task boards** and **Activity** (`?tab=` keys `members`, `settings`, `cv-storage`, `speed`, `task-boards`, `activity`; anything else opens Members), still opened only through `teamPageAccess()` on the server. CV storage, Speed and Task boards render today's screens and Activity the team's own activity feed (`GET /api/v1/activity`, shared with `/app/activity`), with unchanged calls, refusals and messages; Settings gains **Prepare task board** under the rename form. Every request of these tabs names the team in the URL: a `TeamScope` gives `useApi`/`useCachedQuery` and `useTeamId()` that team (its own cache beside the active team's, which an identity change empties of other teams) and writes go through `requestJson(teamId, …)`; opening the page never changes the active team. The page table loses `members`, `cv-storage`, `speed` and `task-boards` (sidebar, Admin home cards and `adminTabs()`); `tokens` keeps its link to `/app/settings/api-tokens`. `/app/admin/members`, `/app/admin/cv-storage`, `/app/admin/speed`, `/app/admin/task-boards` (in `[tab]/page.tsx`, from `OLD_TEAM_PAGES`), `/app/settings/team` and `/app/settings/speed` redirect an owner or admin of the current team to the matching tab through one helper, `redirectToTeamTab()`; anyone else gets the page's previous outcome in place. `/app/admin/tokens` and `/app/admin/history` are not redirected. Personal settings list only My profile, API tokens and About, and the **Admin portal** link follows `adminTabs()` over `GET /api/v1/me/access`, never the team role. Team & access keeps Your teams, the current team's name and role, read-only members (with the company owner's Configured role column) and Create a team. No API route, migration, MCP, role-template or `teamPageAccess()`/`adminPageAccess()` rule change; no row status changes.
- **Files touched:** `src/components/settings/team-detail-tabs.ts` (new: `TEAM_DETAIL_TABS`, `TeamDetailTab`, `teamDetailTab`), `src/components/settings/team-detail.tsx` (six tabs in a `TeamScope`, the Settings tab with Prepare task board, the CV storage tab's busy state and notices, `TeamDetail` keyed by the active team), `src/components/workspace/client.tsx` (`TeamScope`, `useTeamId`, `useQueryScope` and a second cache for scoped reads, cleared with the workspace cache and refreshed after writes), `src/components/workspace/overview.tsx` (`ActivityPanel` shared by `ActivityScreen` and the new `ActivityList`), `src/components/settings/storage-settings.tsx` (`useTeamId`, `saveStorageSettings`), `src/components/settings/speed-settings.tsx` (`SpeedTab`; `SpeedSettings` is the refusal page; `SpeedPanel` has a heading), `src/components/settings/task-boards-tab.tsx` (`useTeamId`, `taskBoardRequests`), `src/components/settings/team-members.tsx` (`prepareTaskBoard` in `teamRequests`), `src/components/settings/admin-tabs.ts` (four rows removed, `OLD_TEAM_PAGES`, `oldTeamPage`, doc comments), `src/components/settings/admin-portal.tsx` (only the API tokens panel; no `task-boards` branch), `src/components/settings/settings-navigation.tsx` (three sections, `canOpenAdminPortal`, `useAdminPortalLink`), `src/components/settings/team-settings.tsx` (no rename, Prepare task board or CV storage), `src/components/settings/profile-settings.tsx`, `token-settings.tsx` and `about-settings.tsx` (the link rule), `src/components/settings/create-team.tsx` (`BOOTSTRAP_FAILED` names the Settings tab), `src/lib/admin-access.ts` (`redirectToTeamTab`), `src/app/app/admin/[tab]/page.tsx`, `src/app/app/admin/teams/[team]/page.tsx`, `src/app/app/settings/team/page.tsx`, `src/app/app/settings/speed/page.tsx`, `scripts/smoke-local.ts` (`settings/about` instead of `settings/team`), `docs/user-guide.md`, `docs/accessibility-checklist.md` and this entry. `drizzle/`, `infrastructure/`, `src/app/api/`, `src/lib/mcp/`, `src/db/`, `scripts/runtime-role-template.ts`, `package.json` and `package-lock.json` are unchanged; no page file was added under `src/app/app/admin`.
- **Tests:** `tests/admin-redirects.test.ts` (new, split-role PGlite with fictional data and the `next/navigation` mock of `tests/admin-routes.test.ts`: each of the six old routes sends a company owner who is team owner, a team owner and a team admin of the current team, the last two also administering another team, to exactly `/app/admin/teams/<current team id>?tab=<tab>` after one `withTeam` in that team; anonymous and Bearer-only callers get `/sign-in` with no `withTeam` or token lookup; a recruiter, a viewer, a company owner who is recruiter in the current team, a company admin who is viewer there and a user with no team get the no-access state on the four admin keys, the speed notice on `/app/settings/speed` and the reduced Team & access on `/app/settings/team`, with Create a team only for the initial administrator; `/app/admin/tokens` and `/app/admin/history` render as before). `tests/admin-routes.test.ts` (`TEAM_PAGES` is `tokens`; every page file is still in the table; the team detail matrix runs for no `?tab=` and each of the six keys, and the allowed props carry the tab). `tests/admin-portal-ui.test.tsx` (seven pages; the `adminTabs()` matrices without the four keys; no card or link for them; only the tokens panel links to settings; the six tabs in order with their ids and `?tab=` selection, `teamDetailTab`; the Settings tab with Prepare task board; the CV storage, Speed, Task boards and Activity markup; `/app/activity`'s list markup equal to the tab's; a recorder of `useApi` and a fake `fetch` showing every read of the four tabs, the vacancy picker of a board form and every write, Prepare task board included, sends `X-Team-Id` of the page's team from the scoped cache, and only the sidebar's access reads the active team; the Admin portal link over team roles × company levels × directory action). `tests/settings-ui.test.tsx` (the navigation lists only My profile, API tokens and About and marks nothing on Team & access and Speed; the Admin portal link by its prop and hidden while access loads; the storage and speed cases against the moved components; Team & access without CV storage, rename or Prepare task board for every role and without a team; the speed refusal). `tests/task-boards.test.ts`, `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` (6, 11 and 15) and `tests/mcp-oauth.test.ts` pass unchanged.
- **Local:** done with Node 24 on PGlite with fictional data, no migration. `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm test` (113 files, 1,782 tests), `npm run test:storage`, `npm run build` and Prettier on every changed file pass in the worktree. In the browser as the fictional seed owner, with the seed team and then a second fictional team current: the six tabs by keyboard and by `?tab=`, a CV storage save, Prepare task board, activity paging, search and filter, each tab showing the page's own team, the six redirects and the unchanged tokens and history pages, the settings navigation, the sidebar and Admin home, and every tab at 1280×800, 768×1024 and 360×740 in both themes; see the accessibility checklist.
- **Owner steps:** none: no migration or setting. Open for review (the spec's assumptions): `/app/settings/team` redirects only owners and admins of the current team, and recruiters, viewers and people with no team keep a reduced Team & access (1, product decision); a refused caller on an old address gets its previous outcome in place (2); the mapping sends `/app/settings/team` to Members (3); the tab order and keys (4); Prepare task board moves to the Settings tab and the create-team failure message names it there (5, product decision); the Activity tab is the existing team activity feed (6); every tab acts on the team in the URL (7); the Admin portal link follows `adminTabs()` and is hidden while access loads (8); the CV contents checkbox stays on CV storage (9); this heading (10). Built this way: scoped reads use their own cache, because the workspace cache drops every other team's entries when its identity is set, which on a full page load happens after the tabs subscribed; the redirects arrive as a streamed redirect (200 with a refresh to the tab, as `/app/settings` already does) because `/app` has a `loading.tsx`; the Speed panel and the Activity tab gained an `h2` like the other tabs; the Settings tab's Prepare task board sits in its own "Task board" panel with one sentence; and `/app/settings/speed` no longer loads timings, since everyone who could see them is redirected.

### ADM-16 Company authority page and two-step for company authority (job 077)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item ADM-16 (backlog `R2-ADM-16`, milestone M3 "Roles and permissions views", epics R2-01 and R2-06; spec IDs PRM-001, PRM-002, PRM-003, PRM-010 and IAM-034; architecture [§14.4](../release-2/architecture.md#144-roles-permission-matrix-and-permission-helper), with §14.1 for the shell and caller matrix and §14.14 for areas; owner decisions D10, D20, D21 and D29, option A of 8 and 10 November 2026). The Company authority panel leaves Roles & permissions for its own page, `/app/admin/company-authority` (table key `company-authority`, level `owner`, first in Access, served by `[tab]/page.tsx` through `adminPageAccess()` and `authorize()`), which lists company owners and admins with their areas (People included), **Plan & pay access**, **Two-step** (On or Off) and **Since** (`granted_at`), and grants, changes and removes them through the same helper, with the job 070 fresh-session dialog and one audit row per change. Plan & pay access is the display name of the per-person `plan_access` grant, one `role="switch"` checkbox whose description says plainly that plan figures and salary brackets do not exist yet, so it opens nothing today. Roles & permissions keeps one sentence, "Company authority has its own page.", with **Open Company authority**. Migration `0043` makes `tenant_context.set_workspace_authority` refuse any level for a target without two-step sign-in (`RQ017`, 409 `authority_two_step_required`, after every earlier refusal; removal always allowed), and every `twoStepSetupRequired` gate now also counts an unsuspended company authority row, so a company owner or admin without two-step is sent to set it up exactly as team owners and admins are. Existing holders without two-step keep their rows. No grantable area, plan figure, policy page or role template change.
- **Files touched:** `drizzle/0043_company_authority_two_step.sql` (new: `CREATE OR REPLACE` of `set_workspace_authority(text, text, text[], boolean)` with the `RQ017` check after `RQ005`; storage schema 21), `src/db/storage-schema.ts`, `src/lib/company-authority.ts` (`RQ017` → 409 `authority_two_step_required`; `twoStep` from the identity query that already loads names and `since` from the existing `withTeam` statement), `src/lib/auth-policy.ts` (`twoStepSetupRequired` takes a required `companyAuthority` input), `src/lib/two-step.ts` (`companyAuthorityHeld`: `tenant_context.install`'s `companyLevel` on the identity pool, only when two-step is off, no team role already requires it and there is a membership), the five gates `src/lib/auth.ts` (`hooks.before`), `src/lib/principal.ts` (`requirePrincipal`), `src/lib/workspace.ts` (`readWorkspace`), `src/app/(auth)/two-step-setup/page.tsx` and `src/app/oauth/authorize/page.tsx`, `src/app/api/openapi.json/route.ts` (`twoStep`, `since` and the 409), `src/components/settings/company-authority-tab.tsx` (new: `CompanyAuthorityTab`, `AuthorityFields`, `CompanyAuthorityPanel` and their helpers, moved from `roles-tab.tsx`; the table uses the `compact` style and the record screens' phone layout), `src/components/settings/roles-tab.tsx` (the panel removed, the link sentence, "Plan & pay access" in the role editor; `box`, `checks`, `actionRow`, `plainError` and `useFocusAfter` exported for the new file), `src/components/settings/admin-tabs.ts`, `src/components/settings/admin-portal.tsx`, `src/domain/config.ts` (the role editor refusal for `plans` says "Plan & pay access"), `scripts/live-role-tests.ts` (`workspace-authority-two-step-required`; the race check's targets get two-step on), `docs/user-guide.md`, `docs/authentication.md`, `docs/security.md`, `docs/identity-boundary-plan.md` (Company authority two-step), `docs/deployment.md` (the `0043` paragraph), `docs/accessibility-checklist.md` and this entry. `infrastructure/`, `src/db/connection-boundary.ts`, `scripts/runtime-role-template.ts`, `tenant_context.install`, `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts`, `tests/runtime-role-grants.test.ts` and `tests/fresh-session.test.ts` are unchanged.
- **Tests:** `tests/workspace-tenancy.test.ts` (new describe on split-role PGlite: direct SQL as the data role under a company owner session refuses `owner` and `admin` for a member without two-step with `RQ017` and no `app.workspace_admins` or `app.activity` change; with two-step on the grant succeeds with one audit row; an owner-written holder without two-step is refused a change and a re-send and is removed with one audit row; `setCompanyAuthority` answers 409 `authority_two_step_required` with the exact message; the leaver fixture gains two-step). `tests/migration-upgrade.test.ts` (from `0042`: a company owner and a company admin with `people` and `plan_access`, both without two-step, keep every column of their rows, the users and activity, marker 21, and later grants are refused with `RQ017`; markers 20 → 21; the `0029`, `0032` and `0041` admin fixtures gain two-step). `tests/auth-flows.test.ts` (new describe on split-role PGlite with Better Auth's handler and fictional members: a recruiter with an unsuspended company admin row and two-step off gets 403 `two_step_setup_required` from `organization/list` and from `requirePrincipal` and a redirect to `/two-step-setup?next=/app` from `getWorkspaceContext()`; with two-step on all three pass; a plain recruiter and a suspended holder without two-step pass; a team owner without two-step is still gated; the data pool is never opened; the two earlier describes now run every migration with the identity role's `install` grant). `tests/two-step-sign-in.test.ts` (same harness change), `tests/identity-pool-routing.test.ts` (the key installed and the routed recruiter without company authority, so the lookup runs and stays on the identity pool). `tests/admin-routes.test.ts` (a company admin with no area; the Company authority matrix: allowed for both company owners, refused for team owner, team A owner, team admin, recruiter, viewer, company admin with no area, fields, organisation and people, and no team; sign-in for anonymous and Bearer with no token lookup). `tests/admin-portal-ui.test.tsx` (eight pages with Company authority first in Access; both `adminTabs()` matrices; the Admin home card; Roles & permissions without the panel and with the link; the eight columns with `data-label`, On/Off, Since, All areas and People; the switch markup; the two-step and last-owner refusals as alerts). `tests/admin-portal-routes.test.ts` (the GET body gains `twoStep` and `since`), `tests/openapi-contract.test.ts`, and marker 21 in `tests/cv-storage-migration.test.ts`, `tests/connection-boundary-boot.test.ts` and `tests/db-pool-preflight.test.ts`. `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` (6, 11 and 15), `tests/runtime-role-grants.test.ts` and `tests/fresh-session.test.ts` pass unchanged.

The cluster was then stopped and deleted. The pooled run was not done: no PgBouncer binary was available and none may be downloaded. In the browser as the fictional seed owner at 1280×800, 768×1024 and 360×740 in both themes: the sidebar and card, the holders table, the refusal by keyboard, an existing holder without two-step refused and removed, and the Roles link; see the accessibility checklist.
- **Owner steps:** apply `0043` after `0042` on Preview (no role template rerun), then `scripts/db-role-evidence.ts` (zero drift) and `npm run test:live-roles`, direct and through the pooler. Until `0043` is applied, hosted startup refuses with 503 `storage_schema_mismatch`. Before deploying, check **Admin portal > Company authority** for holders shown with two-step Off: they keep their authority but are sent to set up two-step at their next request. Open for review (the spec's assumptions): the page is the `company-authority` table key, first in Access (1); Since is `granted_at`, reset by every grant, change or restore (2); the check refuses any non-null level, re-sends, area or Plan & pay access changes and restores included, after `RQ006` and `RQ005`, and never a removal (3); `RQ017` maps to 409 `authority_two_step_required` (4); the user row is read without a lock (5); all five gates count company authority (6); the lookup reads `install`'s `companyLevel` on the identity pool, costing one extra identity transaction per gated request for a member with two-step off and no owner or admin team role, a trade-off for the owner to confirm (7); a suspended row does not gate (8); Plan & pay access is a `role="switch"` checkbox, Yes/No in the table, Two-step On/Off (9); the switch's wording (10); Roles keeps a link, not a redirect (11); `0043` and marker 21 (12). Built this way: the auth harnesses of `tests/auth-flows.test.ts` and `tests/two-step-sign-in.test.ts` now apply every migration (`applyWorkspaceSchema` in `tests/helpers/auth.ts`), since a recruiter without two-step now reaches the lookup; the role editor's refusal for the plans resource was renamed to Plan & pay access with the rest of the UI; a refused save leaves focus on the page, as the panel did before.

### ADM-05 Team archive and restore (job 078)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item ADM-05 (backlog `R2-ADM-05`, milestone M1 "Admin shell and Teams move", MVP order 1, the last M1 item, epic R2-01; spec ID DOM-005; architecture [§14.2](../release-2/architecture.md#142-teams-moves-into-the-admin-portal) "Archive and restore a team (DOM-005)", with §14.1 for the shell; depends on ADM-04; owner decisions D1, D20 and D28, option A of 8 and 10 November 2026). A team owner archives their own team on the new **Archive** tab of `/app/admin/teams/[team]` (seventh and last, `?tab=archive`) after typing its name, and restores it with **Restore team**; a team admin sees the state and "Only a team owner can archive or restore this team." with no button. Migration `0044` adds `archived_at` and `archived_by` to `app.workspace_teams`, the owner-run `tenant_context.set_team_archived(text, boolean)` (session owner of that team only, `42501` otherwise, one `team-archive` activity row per change, `changed` false and no row for a repeat), a new `install` body that stores only the `:read` scopes of every context of an archived team and returns `teamArchived`, a new `allows_workspace` body whose task-board write branch needs the context's team not to be archived, the owner-only `team-archive` branch of the activity insert policy, an owner-only INSERT policy on `app.storage_usage` (see Owner steps), `workspace_team_list()` with an `archived` column and the read helper `tenant_context.member_archived_teams()` (session contexts; the caller's own archived teams of the workspace). The service marks the principal in `withTeam` and answers every write with 403 `team_archived`, "This team is archived. You can still read everything, but nothing can be changed until a team owner restores it.", from `requireScope` (a missing `:write` scope), `authorize()` (any action but view and export), the task-board writes and MCP write tools (audited as `denied`; `tools/list` lists only read tools). `PUT /api/v1/teams/archive` (`{archived}` → `{archived, changed}`, 403 `team_archive_forbidden` for anyone but a session owner of the team) and `GET /api/v1/me/archived-teams` are new; `GET /api/v1/me/access` gains `teamArchived` and `GET /api/v1/teams` rows `archived`. The switcher labels an archived team "<name> (Archived)", the Teams list shows **Archived** after its name, and the shell shows one status notice while the active team is archived. Nothing is deleted.

Built this way, beyond the spec: `0044` also gives the owner an INSERT policy on `app.storage_usage`, as `0007` gave it SELECT and UPDATE, because the CV storage settings helpers create the usage row with `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, which the write gate of an archived team's read-only context refused, so Requirement 12's CV storage settings would otherwise fail (every helper that inserts the row checks its caller first, and the `storage_usage_owner_only` trigger still keeps runtime roles from writing the table; a security review point); a recruiter's team bootstrap keeps its earlier role refusal, which comes first; API tokens get 403 `session_required` from the session-only routes (`me/access`, task boards, bootstrap), archived or not; and after a change the page reloads on `?tab=archive`, since switching tabs does not put the tab in the address.

### ADM-24 Job families, titles and grades (job 079)

- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item ADM-24 (backlog `R2-ADM-24`, milestone M4 "Org tree and job architecture", MVP order 4, epic R2-14; spec IDs ORG-102, ORG-104 and ORG-105; architecture [§14.5](../release-2/architecture.md#145-org-tree-and-job-architecture-org-101-to-org-107) "Job families, job titles and job grades", with §1.2 and §1.3 for the configuration store and versioning; depends on ADM-01; owner decisions D1, D13, D20 and D22, option A of 8 and 10 November 2026). The company job architecture is one versioned configuration object of the new type `job-architecture`, always key `company`, written under the existing `organisation` area: company owners and company admins with `organisation`, signed in, create a draft, edit it and publish numbered versions through the existing store, with one `config.draft_created` and one `config.published` row each; everyone else gets 403 `config_forbidden`, and `42501` by direct SQL as the data role. Its strict body (`jobArchitectureBodySchema`) holds families `{key, name}` (at most 200), grades `{code, name, order}` (at most 200; unique code of 1 to 20 upper-case letters, digits and hyphens; unique order 1 to 1000) and titles `{key, name, familyKey, gradeCode}` (at most 2,000; unique key; family and grade of the same body), names, codes and order only, with no pay key. Every member reads the published version (`GET /api/v1/job-architecture`; the draft only for editors). Positions take a `jobTitleKey` of the published version (`app.positions.job_title_key`), and vacancies an optional `data.jobTitleKey` system field, a configuration key checked when set or changed; an unknown key, or any key before a version is published, is 400 `invalid_reference`. The default vacancy kind schema shows the field as the read-only "Job title", so the workspace editor neither renders nor sends it. REST, OpenAPI and MCP vacancy shapes gain only that optional field; the MCP tool list and the `tenant_context.audit_mcp_call` allowlist are unchanged, and no tool returns job architecture data. No pay data, no new area, grant, table, helper or `install` change, and no row status changes.
- **Files touched:** `drizzle/0045_job_architecture.sql` (new: `app.positions.job_title_key` with `positions_job_title_key_check`; `config_objects_type_check` with `job-architecture`; the twelve runtime `context_*` policies of the three configuration tables recreated with `job-architecture` → `organisation`; `tenant_context.audit_config`, `publish_config_version` and `retire_config_version` and `app.audit_directory_change()` replaced with `CREATE OR REPLACE`; storage schema 23), `src/db/storage-schema.ts`, `src/domain/record-schemas.ts` (`configKey` moved here and re-exported from `config.ts`; `vacancy.jobTitleKey`), `src/domain/config.ts` (`job-architecture` in `CONFIG_TYPES` and `CONFIG_TYPE_AREAS`, `JOB_ARCHITECTURE_KEY`, `jobArchitectureBodySchema`, `JobArchitectureBody`), `src/domain/config-defaults.ts` ("Job title", read-only), `src/domain/directory.ts` (`positionInput` and `positionFacts`), `src/lib/config-store.ts` (`mayWriteConfig` exported from `requireWrite`; the `company` key check in `createConfigDraft`), `src/lib/job-architecture.ts` (new: `jobArchitectureFor`, `getJobArchitecture`, `requireJobTitle`), `src/lib/directory.ts` (`Position.jobTitleKey`, `positionJson`, `createPosition`, `updatePosition`), `src/lib/records.ts` (`createInside` and `updateRecord` for vacancies), `src/app/api/v1/job-architecture/route.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/job-architecture/[versionId]/route.ts` and `src/app/api/v1/job-architecture/[versionId]/publish/route.ts` (new, `humanOnly`, no fresh session), `src/app/api/v1/positions/[id]/route.ts` (doc comment), `src/app/api/openapi.json/route.ts` (the four operations, `jobTitleKey` on positions), `src/components/settings/admin-portal.tsx` ("Job architecture" in the change list), `docs/api-examples.md`, `docs/user-guide.md`, `docs/identity-boundary-plan.md` (the type list, the area mapping and Job architecture), `docs/deployment.md` (the `0045` paragraph) and this entry. `infrastructure/postgres-split-runtime-roles.sql`, `scripts/runtime-role-template.ts`, `src/db/connection-boundary.ts`, `src/lib/org-import.ts`, `src/lib/mcp/` and `tests/round-trip-budget.test.ts` are unchanged.

Open for review (the spec's assumptions): the fixed key `company` (1); grade codes of 1 to 20 upper-case letters, digits and hyphens and a unique order of 1 to 1000 (2); names need not be unique (3); the draft only for editors and session-only routes (4); title keys checked by the service, the database checking only their format (5); vacancy keys checked only when set or changed (6); a vacancy's key can be changed but not cleared (7); the vacancy default marks the field read-only, so vacancy exports gain an empty "Job title" column for existing rows (8); `get_vacancy` shows `jobTitleKey: null` for a vacancy without one (9); no fresh session to publish (10). Built this way: `configKey` moved from `src/domain/config.ts` to `src/domain/record-schemas.ts` (re-exported unchanged), because the vacancy schema needs it and `config.ts` already imports that file; the key check runs after the write check, so a caller without authority gets 403 `config_forbidden` whatever the key; and `mayWriteConfig` is the write rule of `requireWrite`, exported for the draft read.

### ADM-08 User detail and invitations to several teams (job 080)

- **Rework:** job 080 reworks job 076, which was merged as `2f282a5` and reverted as `46fbb78` because one source assertion of its keyboard test compared a literal with LF line breaks against a working copy checked out with CRLF (`core.autocrlf=true`). This job reapplies 076 on top of jobs 077 to 079, renumbers its migration to `0046` (storage schema 24), makes that assertion a whitespace-agnostic regex, and points the Company authority tab at the Company authority page of job 077.
- **Requirements covered:** Release 2 build item ADM-08 (backlog `R2-ADM-08`, milestone M2 "Users and groups", epic R2-13; spec IDs IAM-005, IAM-072 and IAM-090; architecture [§14.3](../release-2/architecture.md#143-users-and-groups-as-company-objects), [§14.1](../release-2/architecture.md#141-admin-shell-and-sitemap-under-appadmin) and [§14.14](../release-2/architecture.md#1414-company-areas-for-the-new-pages); owner decisions D1, D20 and D29, option A of 8 and 10 November 2026). `/app/admin/users/[user]` (a sub-page of Users: company owners and company admins holding `people`, checked on the server through `adminPageAccess()` and `adminTabs()`, `?tab=` with Profile as the fallback) shows the person's name and five tabs: **Profile** (name, email, Active, last sign-in), **Teams & roles** (team, team role, configured role or "Team role only"), **Company authority** (read only: level, areas by display name, Plan & pay access, since, Suspended; the link "Change on Company authority" to `/app/admin/company-authority` for a company owner only, a plain sentence otherwise), **Security** (two-step On or Off, signed-in devices, and **Reset two-step sign-in** only for someone else with two-step on in a team the caller owns, through today's team-owner reset with the confirmation, the `SESSION_NOT_FRESH` dialog and one retry) and **Activity** (up to 50 configuration changes the person made and access changes made to them, in plain words, "This person" for their own). Users list names link to it; invited rows do not. **Invite to teams** on Users offers only the caller's teams where they are team owner or admin, archived teams included because invitations keep working there (job 078), and sends one ordinary Better Auth invitation per chosen team, with a per-team outcome; Better Auth refuses a forged team as today, and nothing in the path sends a company level, area, plan access or configured role. The owner-run helper `tenant_context.workspace_person` returns no team id, record, candidate, upload, storage, token, session, IP or user agent data and nothing outside the context's workspace; the session-only `GET /api/v1/admin/users/{user}` (403 `session_required` for tokens, 403 `people_forbidden` for everyone `authorize()` or the helper refuses, 404 `user_not_found` outside the workspace) serves the page. No deactivate, sign out everywhere, clear lockout, anonymise, groups, effective access, bulk invite, edit, resend or cancel control (ADM-09 onwards).

- **Owner steps:** apply `0046` after `0045` on Preview, rerun the role template, then `scripts/db-role-evidence.ts` (zero drift) and `npm run test:live-roles`, direct and through the pooler. Until `0046` is applied, hosted startup refuses with 503 `storage_schema_mismatch`; until the template is rerun, the user detail page answers 403 `people_forbidden`. Open for review (the spec's assumptions): a migration although the build plan said none (1); one helper row with up to 50 events (2); Activity is the person's configuration changes and access changes made to them (3); events carry the actor's display name (4); team slugs are returned to match the caller's own teams for Reset (5); one Reset button that names one team the caller owns (6); Reset hidden when two-step is off (7); the Company authority link for company owners only (8); anonymous and token callers go to sign-in on the page (9); the invite form on the Users list calls Better Auth once per team from the browser, and sent invitations stand on a partial failure (10); status is always Active (11); the live checks were extended (12). Built this way: a company owner's areas read "All areas" and an admin without areas "No areas", as in the Company authority panel; plan access shows the stored value, also for a company owner; a configuration event reads "<change> · <type>: <key>", for example "Published · Workflow: hiring-flow"; the invite role choices use the list's labels (Owner, Admin, Recruiter, Viewer) and default to Recruiter; Better Auth ignores extra body fields on `invite-member` (200 with the plain invitation), which the test now pins.

### WP-16 MCP endpoint

- **Requirements covered:** SRS-API-01..05, SRS-TOK-01..04, SRS-SEC-06, SRS-SEC-07, SRS-AI-06, BRD FR-ADM-02
- **Files touched:** `src/app/api/mcp/route.ts`, `src/lib/mcp/` (`server.ts`, `tools.ts`, `results.ts`), `src/lib/oauth.ts`, `src/lib/api-tokens.ts`, `src/lib/principal.ts`, `src/app/oauth/`, `src/app/.well-known/`, `src/app/api/oauth/consent/route.ts`, `src/components/auth/oauth-consent.tsx`, `src/components/settings/token-settings.tsx`, `drizzle/0010_mcp_oauth.sql`, `infrastructure/postgres-split-runtime-roles.sql`, `docs/mcp.md`
- **Local:** done. Stateless Streamable HTTP MCP server (pinned `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk`) with 8 read tools and 2 idempotent write tools; API-token and minimal in-repo OAuth 2.1 (PKCE S256, rate-limited dynamic registration, consent page, grants as `auth.api_tokens` rows, 1-hour audience-bound access tokens, rotating refresh tokens); per-call `mcp-tool` audit through an owner-run helper. Tested in `tests/mcp-endpoint.test.ts` and `tests/mcp-oauth.test.ts`.
- **BLOCKERS:** the owner applies migration `0010` and the role template to Preview, reruns `scripts/db-role-evidence.ts` and `npm run test:live-roles`, and tests live Claude.ai and Claude Code connections on a deployment. Owner decisions noted in the job spec: Better Auth plugin versus in-repo server (A1), grant and refresh lifetimes (A3), a `claude.ai` redirect-host allowlist (A5) and returning contact fields over MCP (A8).

### WP-16b MCP endpoint hardening

- **Requirements covered:** SRS-TOK-04, SRS-CAN-03, SRS-SEC-06, SRS-API-01, BRD FR-ADM-02
- **Files touched:** `src/lib/oauth.ts`, `src/lib/client-name.ts`, `src/app/oauth/authorize/page.tsx`, `src/components/auth/oauth-consent.tsx`, `src/lib/scope-descriptions.ts`, `src/app/api/openapi.json/route.ts`, `src/lib/mcp/tools.ts`, `src/lib/records.ts`, `src/domain/records.ts`, `src/db/connection-boundary.ts`, `drizzle/0011_oauth_reuse_audit.sql`, `infrastructure/postgres-split-runtime-roles.sql`, docs
- **Local:** done. A5 decided: registration accepts only `https://claude.ai/api/mcp/auth_callback`, `https://claude.com/api/mcp/auth_callback` (exact strings) and `http:` loopback; authorize refuses older clients outside that list and shows an error page with the OAuth error code for every invalid request instead of redirecting. Client names lose control, format and invisible characters at registration and on the consent page. A8 narrowed: MCP no longer returns `explicitGender` or `nationalServiceStatus`, drops evidence entries that mention gender or national service, and its search ignores those fields and `rawSource`, `evidence` and `sourceReviews`; email and phone are still returned. A reused code or refresh token writes one `token.revoked` row (`authorization_code_reused` or `refresh_token_reused`). Consent and OpenAPI OAuth scopes use MCP-accurate wording (`mcpScopeDescriptions`). Tested in `tests/mcp-oauth.test.ts`, `tests/mcp-endpoint.test.ts`, `tests/token-role-boundary.test.ts`, `tests/runtime-role-grants.test.ts`, `tests/connection-boundary.test.ts` and `tests/openapi-contract.test.ts`.
- **BLOCKERS:** the owner applies migration `0011` and the updated role template to Preview, reruns `scripts/db-role-evidence.ts` and `npm run test:live-roles`. Whether `residenceEmirate` should also be excluded from MCP is an open owner decision (spec A1).

### WP-16c ChatGPT MCP compatibility

- **Requirements covered:** SRS-TOK-01, SRS-SEC-06, SRS-API-01, BRD FR-ADM-02
- **Files touched:** `src/lib/oauth.ts`, `src/app/api/openapi.json/route.ts`, `tests/mcp-oauth.test.ts`, `docs/mcp.md`, `docs/authentication.md`, `docs/security.md`, `docs/requirements/register.md`, `README.md`
- **Local:** done. RFC 9207 issuer identification: one `issuer()` helper gives the metadata `issuer`, the protected-resource `authorization_servers` entry and the `iss` parameter on approve and deny redirects; the metadata advertises `authorization_response_iss_parameter_supported: true`. The exact redirect allowlist adds ChatGPT's stable `https://chatgpt.com/connector_platform_oauth_redirect`; the callback-ID form and every look-alike are refused. `resource` (RFC 8707) must equal `<origin>/api/mcp` at authorize and at both token grants when sent (a missing value is accepted and bound to the same audience, spec A2), and the refresh grant now also requires the refresh token's `/api/mcp` audience. Dynamic client registration stays the only registration method; Client ID Metadata Documents are not implemented and the server makes no outbound fetch. No migration. Tested in `tests/mcp-oauth.test.ts`, including end-to-end flows for fake Claude, ChatGPT and loopback clients.
- **BLOCKERS:** live ChatGPT and Claude connection testing on a deployment, and owner verification of which ChatGPT plans or workspace settings allow custom connectors. Making `resource` mandatory is an open owner decision (spec A2).
